WESTPAC ECONOMICS
In China, policymakers announced another round of targeted
support measures aimed at stabilising growth and supporting
the struggling property sector. The PBOC lowered rates on its
Pledged Supplementary Lending facility by 25bps to 1.5% and
expanded quotas for targeted lending programs. Authorities

also introduced subsidies for eligible first-home buyers. The
subsidy applies to homes with a floor area of 120m² or less
and a value below CNY1.5mn, with support capped at 1ppt of
mortgage costs, up to CNY10,000 and for a maximum term of
five years. The measures complement reforms announced in
August to improve developer financing conditions and support
housing completion. However, weak credit demand and
confidence remains a significant constraint, suggesting policy

transmission is still limited.
The September PMI data pointed to a modest improvement
in activity, although the details were less convincing.
Manufacturing returned to expansionary territory, rising 0.9pts
to 50.1, supported by stronger production and new orders.
Much of the improvement, however, reflected a rebound in
prices, with the raw materials price index rising 7.6pts and
producer prices increasing 6.2pts. Labour market conditions
remained weak, with the employment index falling a further

0.6pts and remaining below 50 for almost four years. Conditions
in the non-manufacturing sector also improved, with the
services PMI lifting from 49.0 to 50.2, driven largely by stronger
construction activity. Input and output prices moved higher,
selling prices returned back above 50, export orders improved
to 48.5 and employment increased 0.5pts, although at 45.9 it
remains firmly in contractionary territory. Overall, the surveys
suggest activity is stabilising and policy support is gaining some

traction, but weak labour demand, subdued credit growth and
soft domestic demand indicate the recovery remains fragile.
In Japan, the September Tankan survey delivered another
constructive signal on the economy and reinforced the case
for further policy normalisation. Business conditions improved
by 2pts for large manufacturers and 5pts more broadly, while
profitability indicators strengthened and capital expenditure
intentions remained elevated. Large manufacturers expect

fixed investment to increase by 11.6% in FY2026, while large
non-manufacturers expect investment growth of 11.2%,
pointing to continued confidence in the medium-term outlook.
Capacity indicators improved further across both
manufacturing and non-manufacturing sectors, with measures
of excess production capacity becoming less negative
and indicating that demand remains sufficiently strong to
keep pressure on existing capacity. Labour shortages also

intensified, with employment conditions becoming more
negative across most industries and firm sizes, pointing to a
progressively tighter labour market. Inflation expectations
remained anchored around 2%, providing further evidence
that Japan is moving towards a more sustainable inflation
environment. Overall, the survey was consistent with an
economy operating close to capacity and supports the view
that a December rate hike remains a realistic possibility.

### Page 5

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
5
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
FOCUS ON NEW ZEALAND
Resilient activity and simmering inflation
While bumpy and uneven, New Zealand’s economic
recovery has continued over the past few months, with
activity showing resilience in the face of continued global
headwinds. Under the surface, however, inflation pressures

are bubbling away, with much of that pressure centred on
parts of the economy that aren’t sensitive to interest rates.
Economic activity getting back on firmer footing
Looking first at recent activity data, the economy has been
finding its feet again after taking a knock in the wake of the
Middle East war. That has been seen most clearly in the jobs
market, with the number of filled jobs rising by 0.9% in the
year to August. While not shooting the lights out, that is still a

noticeable improvement from the early part of the year, when
filled job numbers were falling.
Under the surface, it’s still a very mixed picture. Agriculture
remains the main engine of growth, with the strength in
commodity export earnings helping to boost incomes,
confidence and spending in regions with strong rural backbones.
That’s supported firm job growth in regions like Canterbury
(where filled job numbers are up 1.9% over the past year), Otago

(+1.5%) and Southland (+3.1%). In contrast, jobs growth remains
much more moderate in Auckland (up 0.6%) and Wellington
(up just 0.4%). That town-and-country split is likely to remain a
feature of the economic landscape for some time yet.
Looking ahead, businesses expect trading conditions to
remain resilient through the final months of the year. That
was reflected in September’s Business Outlook survey.
While the survey’s gauges of expected business activity and

general business confidence eased over the past month, they
remain at firm levels, consistent with continued moderate
growth through the latter part of the year. That resilience has
also been evident in the household sector, with consumer
confidence largely steady through September.
The resilience in household and business sentiment is notable,
as fuel prices have taken a big step higher over the past month.
The average price of 91 unleaded has risen by around 49c/litre –

albeit late in the month and likely not fully reflected in surveyed
confidence – taking it back to the levels seen during the early
stages of the Middle East conflict. Those higher fuel prices
have taken a bite out of household disposable incomes, with
spending on fuel up about 22% compared with this time last
year, even though the volume of fuel sold is down around 11%.
But even with the lift in fuel prices, we haven’t seen the same
degree of pessimism as earlier in the year, when fuel prices first

spiked. Notably, those higher fuel prices haven’t spilled over
into a broader lift in output price pressures either (at least at
this stage). Inflation expectations were steady in September’s
household and business surveys. Similarly, the number of firms
planning to raise prices fell back despite a lift in wage and cost
expectations. However, while that stability in pricing gauges is
welcome, measures of inflation pressure remain elevated.
Taken together, recent data reinforce indications that the

economy is shaking off the impacts of the Iran war.
Persistent inflation drivers and the RBNZ
Turning to inflation, the spike in oil prices earlier this year saw
annual inflation climb to 4.1% in the year to June. With local
fuel costs rising again in recent weeks, we continue to expect
inflation to remain above 3% until at least mid-2027.
However, even before the recent rise in fuel costs, inflation had
been running hot for an extended period. Excluding the rise

in food and fuel prices, inflation in the year to June was 2.9%,
and it’s been running close to or above 3% for five years now.
As we discuss in our recent report, much of the strength in
inflation over the past year has been related to administered
costs, such as local council rates, or to sectors that are less
exposed to competitive pressures, such as electricity and
insurance. Those increases have persisted for an extended period,
and we expect further sizeable increases in many of these costs.

This ongoing firmness is a key reason why we expect domestic
(non-tradables) inflation to be higher than the RBNZ expects
over the longer term. It’s also part of the reason why we
expect the RBNZ will ultimately need to raise the OCR by
more than it assumed in its September policy statement.
We are forecasting a 25bp rate rise at the RBNZ’s December
meeting and expect the OCR to peak at 4% next year.
This is a tough environment for the RBNZ. With ongoing

increases in administered prices boosting core inflation at a
time when energy prices are driving headline inflation well
above the target range, it’s hard for the RBNZ to look through
them. The RBNZ had little headroom to absorb upside inflation
surprises, even before the recent spike in oil prices.
Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist

### Page 6

the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
6
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
But getting inflation back to target in the face of an oil price
spike and high administered prices will not be easy and involves
some tough trade-offs. Administered prices tend to be less
responsive to changes in economic conditions. As a result,
interest rate hikes are likely to have a bigger impact on parts of

the economy that are not the main drivers of inflation. Tighter
policy will also weigh on the labour market and near-term
growth, while increasing households’ debt-servicing costs.
This is an uncomfortable but necessary trade-off. Looking
through, or not responding to, these sorts of large and persistent
cost increases would still leave households facing significant
increases in overall living costs. In the long run, it would be
more damaging for households and the economy if generalised

inflation were to become embedded in price setting behaviour.
The Kiwi has lost its wings
Adding to near-term inflation risks has been the drop in the NZ
dollar. On a trade-weighted basis, the Kiwi has fallen around
4% since the start of September, and it’s begun the December
quarter around 4% below the level the RBNZ assumed in its
September policy statement.
The slide in the Kiwi is mainly due to the strength of the US
dollar, with NZD/USD down nearly 5% since the start of

September to sit just below 0.56 at the time of writing. Moves
against other currencies have been more modest. For example,
NZD/AUD is down 2% over the same period.
Technically, NZD/USD seems oversold. In the short term,
trends in the US dollar will dominate, including any response
to the US labour market report due on Friday evening. Any
turnaround in the USD would likely see NZD/USD trade back
towards the top of the 0.5580–0.5680 range that is governing

the pair for the time being.
NZD/AUD will continue to be driven by changing views on
interest rate differentials between the RBNZ and RBA. This
week, NZD/AUD corrected higher from around 0.8070 to as high
as 0.8110 as RBA cash rate expectations were repriced following
the RBA Board meeting and Australian CPI. In the short term, the
bias for NZD/AUD could be lower, given our view that the RBNZ
will pause at its late-October meeting and that the market is

under-pricing the risk of an RBA rate hike in November.
Next week’s QSBO, due on Tuesday, will be the key event
risk, with the inflation and growth gauges closely watched for
signs that might prompt the RBNZ to pause or hike. Markets
had previously priced in a high chance of a hike at the RBNZ’s
October meeting, but in recent days appear to be reassessing
that view.  Pricing for a 25bp hike in New Zealand could be
pared back towards a 50:50 call. The 0.8030–0.8150 range

is likely to contain the pair in the immediate period ahead.
Our medium-term view is for NZD/AUD to rise as the RBNZ
increases the OCR to 4% by the second half of 2027. We’ll
update our views on the NZD and other currencies in our next
Market Outlook, out Wednesday 14 October.
Fiscal update
The big uncertainty on the domestic horizon is, of course,
November’s general election, and at this stage the polling
looks tight. Neither the established left nor right bloc has a

clear majority.
Ahead of the election, the Treasury has released its Pre-election
Economic and Fiscal Update (PREFU), updating the Budget
2026 (BEFU) fiscal forecasts to reflect the latest economic and
fiscal information and a revised economic outlook. This allows
the public to assess the state of the Government’s finances. It
also provides other parties with a baseline against which to
calibrate their spending and taxation policies.
The PREFU revealed a stronger starting point for the

Government’s finances than had been expected in the Budget.
• A deficit of $8.6bn is estimated for 2025/26, down from the
Budget estimate of $11.9bn.
• Similarly, the forecast deficit for 2026/27 has been revised
down significantly to $6.8bn from $11.4bn.
• Thereafter, the OBEGALx deficit is expected to continue
declining gradually, moving to a surplus of $4bn in 2028/29.
This broadly follows the Budget profile, but is stronger
across the forecast period.

• Net core Crown debt is forecast to peak at 43.9% of GDP in
2027/28, below the 46.1% peak forecast in the BEFU. It’s then
forecast to decline to 39.5% of GDP by the end of the forecast
period in 2030/31, in line with the Government’s goal of
reducing debt below 40% of GDP over the medium term.
Given the improved outlook, NZDM announced a $4bn
reduction in the 2026/27 bond programme to $30bn and a
$15bn reduction in the forecast programme across the whole

period through to 2029/30.
The medium-term forecasts in the PREFU should be treated
with caution. The economic forecasts underpinning the
PREFU were finalised on 21 August, when both oil prices
and, especially, bond yields were materially lower than they
are today. Had the forecasts been finalised more recently, the
fiscal projections presented today would have been weaker,
at least in the near term. Indeed, the Treasury is upfront in
acknowledging that, while the risks are two-sided, the balance

is skewed to the downside.
Aside from the obvious uncertainty surrounding the economic
outlook because of unpredictable developments in the Middle
East, the approaching General Election, and the possibility of
a change in the composition of the Government, means the
assumptions underpinning the PREFU forecasts could quickly
become invalidated.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
AUS: Oct Westpac-MI Consumer Sentiment (index)
Oct 6, Last: 84.4
Consumer sentiment declined 5.2% to 84.4 in September,
taking the index back towards the deeply pessimistic levels seen
earlier in the year. Rising fuel prices and fears of an imminent

interest rate rise drove the move with a continued weakening in
housing markets likely adding to unease in some quarters.
Developments over the last month would have added to these
concerns with: the RBA raising the cash rate by 25bps to 4.6%
during the survey week; average pump prices pushing above
$2.36/litre nationally and no end in sight to global supply
disruptions;  and more weak updates on the housing market.
NZ: Q2 NZIER Survey of Business Opinion (index)

Oct 6, General business confidence last: 12.3
Business conditions picked up modestly in the June quarter as
concerns about the war in the Middle East eased. However,
cost and pricing gauges remained firm. The September quarter
survey will be closely watched ahead of the RBNZ’s 28
October meeting. Recent weeks have seen signs that activity
is firming. However, fuel prices have also risen sharply, and we
(along with the RBNZ) will be watching for signs of a broader

lift in inflation pressures. The timing of survey responses
within the sample period will also warrant scrutiny, given that
oil prices rose late in the quarter.

## Document 266: Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, September (2026-10-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, September (2026-10-22).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 12
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#businessconditions #confidence #aud #australia
Australian Business Conditions and Confidence, September
By Luka Belobrajdic
Economist, Westpac
14:15 October 13 2026 Share
Confidence posts its sharpest fall since the start of the Middle East conflict as elevated fuel
prices and higher interest rates weigh on business sentiment, while conditions hold steady
at weak levels. Conditions unchanged at -1 / Confidence -4pts to -13.

### Page 2

The September NAB Business Survey was in the field from 23 to 30 September, capturing ongoing
global uncertainty, still-rising fuel costs and, at least in part, the RBA’s September rate hike. Business
confidence continued to deteriorate, recording its sharpest fall since March and the onset of the Middle
East conflict. The index declined 4pts to -13 and, while remaining above the lows recorded earlier this
year, is now at its weakest level since May. At 18pts below its long-run average, the result underscores

the deep pessimism that continues to weigh on businesses. This broadly mirrors the pessimism evident
in the Westpac-Melbourne Institute Consumer Sentiment Index, where elevated fuel prices and higher
interest rates have coalesced in a similarly weak assessment of the outlook.
The headline business conditions index held steady in negative territory at -1, albeit rising 0.25pts on
an unrounded basis. While encouraging that conditions have not deteriorated further, the index has

now remained at -1 (rounded) for two consecutive months, its weakest level since the height of the
COVID-19 pandemic in 2020. At 7pts below its long-run average, the result points to continued
pressure across the business sector.
Across the major sub-components, employment moved into negative territory, falling 3pts to -1 and
sitting 4pts below its long-run average. This marks the first outright negative reading since November
2020 and provides further evidence of the gradual easing in labour market conditions. Against the

backdrop of compressed margins, the result may also indicate firms are becoming increasingly
cautious around hiring decisions. By contrast, trading conditions rose 1pt to 4, although they remain
8pts below their long-run average. The relative resilience in trading conditions is broadly consistent with

### Page 3

Westpac DataX Card-Tracker data, which suggests nominal spending remained resilient, albeit
slowing, through the September quarter despite ongoing headwinds facing consumers. Profitability
recorded the strongest improvement across the major components, rising 3pts to -5, although the sub-
index remains deeply subdued at 10pts below its long-run average.
Capacity utilisation edged 0.2ppt lower to 82.3%. While the monthly decline provides a tentative signal

of easing capacity pressures, utilisation remains 0.9ppt above its long-run average and continues to
trend higher on a three-month average basis. Capital expenditure continued to recover from its mid-
year weakness, rising 3pts to 7. While still below the highs recorded in the first quarter, the sub-index is
now trending higher on a three-month average basis, pointing to some stabilisation in investment
intentions.
Cost growth was comparatively subdued in September, although purchase cost pressures remain

elevated. Purchase cost growth edged 0.1ppt higher to 2.4%qtr, sitting 1.1ppt above its long-run
average and continuing to weigh on margins. By contrast, labour cost growth eased 0.1ppt to 1.7%qtr,
although it remains 0.5ppt above its long-run average. Final product price growth was unchanged at
0.8%qtr, 0.2ppt above its long-run average. The widening gap between purchase cost and final product
price growth suggests firms continue to absorb a meaningful share of higher input costs, consistent

with profitability remaining deeply below average. Looking ahead, the October survey will provide an
early indication of how the card surcharging ban is affecting business costs and the extent to which
these are passed through to final prices.

### Page 4

Related articles
Among other indicators, forward orders declined 1pt to -1, pointing to continued caution around the
demand outlook. Inventories, by contrast, rose 4pts (rounded) to 7, some 5pts above their long-run
average. In combination with weak forward orders, the inventory build points to a degree of slack
emerging across the business sector.

Doanh thu 2Q2026 đạt 1,222 tỷ (-8% YoY/18.1% QoQ) và LNST công ty mẹ đạt 325 tỷ 
VND (7% YoY/55% QoQ), ghi nhận tăng trưởng của tất cả các mảng. Mảng cung cấp 
nước tăng trưởng ổn định, tiếp tục là trụ cột chính của BWE, đóng góp doanh thu 
785 tỷ VND (+12% YoY/12% QoQ) nhờ nhà máy nước Long An vận hành từ Q3/2025 
giúp sản lượng nước tăng 28% YoY.

Cường độ và thời gian kéo dài của các đợt nắng nóng trong 2026/2027 là cơ sở để 
chúng tôi điều chỉnh tăng tăng trưởng tiêu thụ nước của nhóm khách hàng dân cư 
tại Bình Dương và Bình Phước trong năm 2026 - 2027 lên mức 7.5/6.5% YoY. Trái lại, 
triển vọng tăng trưởng tiêu thụ nước nhóm sản xuất ảm đạm do tình hình thuế 
quan có diễn biến bất lợi trong ngắn hạn cho các ngành xuất khẩu truyền thống 
của Việt Nam.

UBND TP.HCM công bố đơn giá thu gom, vận chuyển và xử lý rác thải mới, tăng 
mạnh từ 50-150% nhằm phản ánh đầy đủ và chính xác hơn các chi phí phát sinh 
trong thực tế. Chúng tôi cho rằng điều này sẽ tác động tích cực với BWE khi các dự 
án của doanh nghiệp ở mảng này sẽ vận hành hiệu quả hơn. Dự báo doanh thu và 
lợi nhuận gộp của mảng xử lý rác thải lần lượt đạt 1,069/1,234 và 165.7/180.7 tỷ 
VND lần lượt trong 2026/2027, tương ứng mức tăng 34%/15% và 27%/9% YoY.

### Page 4

4

Tiêu thụ nước khu vực dân sinh 
được thúc đẩy nhờ thời tiết nắng 
nóng hơn; trong khi triển vọng 
nhóm khách hàng công nghiệp bị 
ảnh hưởng bởi bất lợi thuế quan 
 Dự báo đồng thuận xác suất xảy ra El Nino ở mức xấp xỉ 100% từ 2H2026 kéo 
dài đến tháng đầu 2027 cho thấy cường độ và thời gian kéo dài của các đợt 
nắng nóng sẽ gay gắt và kéo dài hơn so với các năm trước. Đây là cơ sở để 
chúng tôi điều chỉnh tăng tăng trưởng tiêu thụ nước nhóm khách hàng dân cư 
tại Bình Dương và Bình Phước trong năm 2026 - 2027 lên mức 7.5/6.5% YoY 
(+2.6/1.1 ppts). Trái lại, sau khi section 301 được công bố và có hiệu lực thay 
thế section 122, tình hình thuế quan có diễn biến bất lợi trong ngắn hạn cho 
các ngành xuất khẩu truyền thống của Việt Nam. Cụ thể, Section 301 không tự 
hết hạn và có nền tảng pháp lý chắc chắn (ít nhất là trong nhiệm kỳ của 
Trump); đồng thời áp dụng hạn ngạch thuế quan cho một lượng hàng dệt may 
nhất định của một số nước không có Việt Nam, khiến bối cảnh bất lợi cho các 
ngành sản xuất truyền thống. Tuy nhiên, với việc Bình Dương đang tập trung 
đầu tư hạ tầng khu công nghiệp hiện đại nhằm thu hút dòng vốn FDI vào các 
lĩnh vực công nghệ cao, sản lượng nước sạch sẽ được duy trì khi tiêu thụ khối 
sản xuất truyền thống được bù đắp bởi nhóm công nghệ cao. Theo đó, trong 
năm 2026, KBSV điều chỉnh tăng nhẹ dự báo sản lượng nước sạch tại Bình 
Dương & Bình Phước đạt 224.9 triệu m3 (+6.8% YoY), với mức tăng của nhóm 
sản xuất/sinh hoạt/hành chính lần lượt là 5.8%/7.5%/11% YoY.

Biểu đồ 4. Xác suất tăng dần của EL Nino

Nguồn: Bloomberg, KBSV tổng hợp

Mảng cấp nước: Tiến độ điều chỉnh 
giá bán nước gián đoạn do môi 
trường lạm phát leo cao 
 Do tác động của cuộc chiến Mỹ - Iran gây ra gián đoạn dòng chảy vận tải dầu 
tại eo biển Hormuz, giá nhiên liệu trong nước đã tăng mạnh từ đầu năm (Dầu 
Diesel - +64.3%, E5 RON 92 – +40%) và khiến cho tình hình lạm phát trong nước 
diễn biến tiêu cực: CPI bình quân 8 tháng năm 2026 tăng 4.5% với chỉ số giá 
nhóm nhà ở, điện nước, chất đốt và vật liệu xây dựng tăng cao nhất với 6.7% và 
giao thông tăng 5.4%. Trong bối cảnh đó, chúng tôi cho rằng tiến độ điều 
chỉnh giá bán nước tại các thị trường của BWE có khả năng bị đẩy lùi sang 
2027-2028 hoặc cho đến khi tình hình giá nhiên liệu ổn định trở lại. Trong dài 
hạn, khả năng đàm phán giá nước thành công là khá cao bởi: i) Thông tư 
145/2025/TT-BTC có hiệu lực từ 2026 là căn cứ để UBND quy định tỷ suất lợi 
nhuận định mức các dự án nhà máy nước; ii) BWE và cổ đông lớn là TDM dự 
kiến đầu tư và mở rộng công suất cung cấp – phân phối nước tại các vùng hẻo 
lánh nên có khả năng tăng áp lực đàm phán tăng giá nước tại Bình Dương. 
Trong kịch bản thận trọng, KBSV điều chỉnh tăng giá nước sạch trung bình khu

vực Bình Dương 1%/năm từ 2028 – 2030.

Đơn giá xử thu gom, vận chuyện và 
xử lý rác thải được phê duyệt cho 
phép cải thiện doanh thu và lợi 
nhuận của mảng này 
 UBND TP.HCM (bao gồm TP.HCM cũ, Bình Dương, Bà Rịa Vũng Tàu) quy định 
giá cụ thể đối với dịch vụ vận chuyển, xử lý chất thải rắn sinh hoạt trên địa bàn. 
Theo đó, quy định mới thay thế văn bản có hiệu lực từ 2016 sẽ chuyển hình 
thức tính giá đầy đủ theo 3 cấu phần: Thu gom, vận chuyển và Xử lý; với khung 
giá được xác định cho các phường đô thị và xã/nông thôn. So với quy định 
2016, quy định mới điều chỉnh tăng mạnh khung giá khoán đối với chủ nguồn 
thải nhỏ (Hộ gia đình tăng 80-184% đối với khu vực đô thị, 55-140% đối với khu 
vực nông thôn; Hộ kinh doanh nhỏ lẻ tăng 42-150% đối với khu vực đô thị, 20-
138% đối với khu vực nông thôn); đồng thời yêu cầu chủ thải tại nguồn và chủ 
nguồn thải lớn áp dụng khung giá thành (bao gồm giá thu gom, vận chuyển và 
xử lý) nhằm phản ánh đầy đủ và chính xác hơn chi phí liên quan tới các khâu xử 
lý rác thải thực tế. Chúng tôi cho rằng việc ban hành khung giá mới tính toán 
đầy đủ và chính xác hơn các chi phí phát sinh phản ánh định hướng phát triển 
hạ tầng xử lý rác thải hiện đại của TP.HCM, đặt trọng tâm vào các nhà máy đốt 
rác phát điện thân thiện và an toàn với môi trường. Đối với doanh nghiệp hoạt 
động trong lĩnh vực môi trường nhiều kinh nghiệm như BWE, cơ chế thu gom, 
vận chuyển và xử lý minh bạch sẽ giúp thúc đẩy hiệu quả các dự án mà doanh 
nghiệp có kế hoạch triển khai trong tương lai. KBSV dự báo doanh thu và lợi 
nhuận gộp của mảng xử lý rác thải lần lượt đạt 1,069/1,234 và 165.7/180.7 tỷ 
VND lần lượt trong 2026/2027, tương ứng mức tăng 34%/15% và 27%/9% YoY.

Biwase thành công tăng vốn cổ 
phần lên 2,513 tỷ VND 
 Sau đợt chào báo cho cổ đông hiện hữu với giá chào bán 37,000 đồng/cổ 
phiếu, doanh nghiệp dự kiến huy động được khoảng 1,162.5 tỷ VND, nâng vốn 
điều lệ lên mức 2,513 tỷ VND. Khoản vốn bổ sung được dự kiến sử dụng cho ba 
mục đích chính: thanh toán nợ vay đến hạn (khoảng 773.3 tỷ đồng), đầu tư dự 
án nhà máy điện rác công suất 24MW (225 tỷ đồng) và mua cổ phần tại CTCP 
Cấp nước Ninh Thuận (hơn 164 tỷ đồng), qua đó biến doanh nghiệp này trở 
thành công ty liên kết. Dự kiến, lợi nhuận đóng góp từ công ty liên kết trong 
2026/2027 tiếp tục tăng trưởng tích cực, đạt 238.1/245.2 tỷ VND (40%/3% YoY).

Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Khuyến nghị MUA với giá mục tiêu 
59,000 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Về diễn biến thị trường, BWE hiện đang giao dịch quanh mức -1 độ lệch chuẩn 
trung bình 5 năm EV/EBITDA, phản ánh những bất ổn trong thương mại toàn 
cầu ảnh hưởng tới tiêu thụ nước phục vụ sản xuất. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi đánh 
giá triển vọng trong tương lai của mảng môi trường (bao gồm nước thải và rác 
thải) cộng với chiến lược đa dạng hóa địa bàn cung cấp nước sẽ bù đắp được 
KQKD tăng trưởng chậm lại.

Kết hợp 2 phương pháp định giá FCFF và EV/EBITDA, KBSV khuyến nghị MUA 
với BWE, giá mục tiêu 59,000 VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương với mức lợi suất kỳ 
vọng 42.2% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 22/09/2026. Mức EV/EBITDA mục tiêu cho 
việc định giá doanh nghiệp là 8.8 lần, tương ứng với mức trung bình 5 năm của 
doanh nghiệp, để phản ánh sự cải thiện KQKD đến từ mảng môi trường và cơ 
chế giảm giá mua nước từ TDM sẽ bù đắp sự giảm tốc của mảng cấp nước trong 
ngắn hạn. Với mức giá mục tiêu trên, cổ phiếu được định giá tương đương P/B 
1.7x cho 2026, phù hợp với việc ROE được kỳ vọng cải thiện lên mức 14.1%.

## Document 323: First Impressions_ NZIER Quarterly Survey of Business Opinion, September quarter 2026 (2026-10-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impressions_ NZIER Quarterly Survey of Business Opinion, September quarter 2026 (2026-10-22).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#businessconditions #newzealand
First Impressions: NZIER Quarterly Survey of Business Opinion,
September quarter 2026
By Michael Gordon
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
08:35 October 06 2026 Share
Businesses are feeling more optimistic about the months ahead, but current conditions
remain tough for many industries. Cost pressures eased off a little compared to last quarter.
Key results (seasonally adjusted)
General business situation: +40 (Prev: +14)

### Page 2

Trading activity, past three months: -1 (Prev: +1) 
Trading activity, next three months: +15 (Prev: +11) 
Average selling prices, past three months: +27 (Prev: +37) 
Average selling prices, next three months: +45 (Prev: +51)

The NZIER’s quarterly survey of business opinion showed a strong lift in general confidence in
September, despite resurgent fuel prices and the uncertainty around the looming election. Firms were
more confident about their prospects for the next few months, even though their recent performance
remained rough in many industries. 
The survey was held over a relatively compressed period (9 to 28 September), with around 40% of
responses coming back on the first day. Later responses were less confident, as the resurgence in
world oil prices was finally reflected in prices at the pump over the course of the month. 
General business sentiment rose to a net 40% positive compared to a net 14% in June, returning to
around the levels seen in the December quarter last year (before the Middle East conflict). However,
the own-activity measures, which tend to have a closer correspondence with GDP growth, were more
subdued. Trading activity over the last three months was slightly weaker than in June, while
expectations for the quarter ahead saw a more modest four-point increase to +15%. 
Other forward-looking measures were also up for the quarter. Hiring intentions rose strongly to a net
23% positive (although past employment was down). Notably, firms continue to report that workers are
becoming harder to find, despite the ongoing rise in unemployment over the last couple of years. That
said, the proportion of firms citing labour as a factor limiting output declined this quarter. Investment
intentions picked up, more so for plant and machinery than for buildings. 
The results were mixed across industries. Manufacturing and services were more upbeat for the
months ahead but reported substantially weaker performance over the last quarter. In contrast, builders
and merchants reported both stronger past results and greater optimism about the near-term outlook. 
The pricing measures in the survey were down compared to June – not entirely surprising as fuel
prices were lower on average through the September quarter compared to the June quarter. The
notable exception was merchants, who expected both higher costs and prices for the quarter ahead. 
The implications for the RBNZ ahead of its 28 October policy review are unclear – there is certainly no
smoking gun in this report that would demand that the RBNZ abandon the “pause” strategy
communicated at the September MPS. Optimism among businesses remains high despite the obvious
headwinds, but there is still a wide gap between hopes and reality. The pricing measures have eased
compared to last quarter, but that may simply confirm the RBNZ’s expectation that annual inflation will
drop from the 4.1% recorded last quarter as fuel prices eased. The September quarter CPI, released
on 22 October, may be more informative in that respect. 
Michael Gordon, Senior Economist
P: +64 21 749 506

## Document 338: How bad is the rise in bond yields_ (2026-09-23)

Source PDF: all report pdf/How bad is the rise in bond yields_ (2026-09-23).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-09-23
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

SPECIAL EDITION | HOW BAS IS THE RISE 
IN BOND YIELDS?

TRANSCRIPTION

0:00:10 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Everyone, welcome. As we head into autumn 2026, interest rates are reaching levels never seen for many, many years. 
Why is this happening? Is it sustainable? And what are the implications for the economies of different countries? All these 
questions will be discussed over the next few minutes with the economists of BNP Paribas. Let us begin with Isabelle 
Mateos y Lago, chief economist of the group. Hello, Isabelle.  
0:00:38 - Isabelle Mateos y Lago 
Hello Emmanuel, hello everyone. 
0:00:39 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Thank you for being with us today. Just to begin, a simple explanation. What rates are we talking about and which countries 
are affected? 
0:00:48 - Isabelle Mateos y Lago 
Yes, it is very important to clarify this. So, we are talking about long-term interest rates, rates at 10 years or longer 
maturities, 30 years, etc. The rates at which sovereign states borrow on the market. And this is primarily something that is 
happening in advanced countries, so United States, United King dom, Japan, and then the Eurozone countries. And indeed, 
as you said, we are seeing since the beginning of the summer, interest rates reach levels we had not seen for about 20 
years. But it is important to keep in mind that up until that point, up until the mid -2000s, the level of interest rates that 
we're seeing now were really quite common, quite normal, so one shouldn't overdramatize.  
0:01:35 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Why do we find ourselves in this situation and how did we get to this point?  
0:01:41 - Isabelle Mateos y Lago 
Yes, so the direct cause is that investors are demanding higher interest rates in order to  lend their capital to governments. 
But why is this? Well, there's essentially three reasons for this, and not all of them are bad, and they're not mutually 
exclusive either. The top reason to my mind is that there's just greater competition for capital be tween sovereign states 
and the corporate sector, who are all facing simultaneously additional investment needs, in defense, in artificial 
intelligence, in energy transition, in resilience. And so,  you just have greater competition for capital and that pushes up the 
price of capital, which is the interest rate. 
Reason number two is because markets have revised upwards their expectations of how high central banks are going to be 
taking their interest rates. 
And this mechanically translates into higher rates at the long end. And then there's a third reason, which doesn't apply 
necessarily in every case, but which is essentially higher sovereign risk, a kind of a risk premium, in cases where there are  
concerns arising about the sustainability of these public debts.

### Page 2

2

0:02:59 - Emmanuel Laborde 
What are the implications for global economies? 
0:03:03 - Isabelle Mateos y Lago 
So that's exactly what we'll be discussing in the next segments. But basically, the two key issues are, one, an adverse 
impact on public finances. It's higher interest rates making it harder to balance the books in terms of public accounts. And 
then secondly, these higher rates tend to slow down economic growth. Of course, by how much and how serious it will be, 
depends on how high interest rates go and how long they stay there. But that's for the next segments.  
0:03:38 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Yes. Thank you very much, Isabelle. Having said the scene now, let's move on to the first segment with the following 
question. What makes debt sustainable? And let's see what Hélène Baudchon has to say.  
Hello Hélène and welcome into this special edition. 
0:03:58 - Hélène Baudchon 
Hello Emmanuel, hello everybody. 
0:04:01 - Emmanuel Laborde 
When economists talk about public debt, they often refer to the sustainability of debt. As an economist, can you explain to 
us what that means and what is the debt trajectory deemed unsustainable?  
0:04:15 - Hélène Baudchon 
To begin with, I will say that a temporary increase in debt can be seen as a normal evolution, not particularly concerning, 
for instance during an economic crisis or more recently during a health crisis. In such cases, it acts as a buffer. A problem  
arises when the debt level never comes back down. An unsustainable trajectory can be therefore defined, characterized by 
a continuous regular rise in the debt level, both in absolute terms and as a proportion of GDP, driven by high and recurring 
fiscal deficits. And sometimes interest payments can exacerbate the problem.  
0:05:00 - Emmanuel Laborde 
In very simple terms, you are talking about the snowball effect.  
0:05:03 - Hélène Baudchon 
Exactly explaining why consolidation measures are needed in order to prevent such an adverse situation from occurring 
and in order to prevent, in fact, a sudden loss of confidence from creditors.  
0:05:16 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Regarding governments, what should serve as a warning sign for them? And at what point could the situation become, let's 
say, problematic? 
0:05:27 - Hélène Baudchon 
Well, simply speaking, when interest rates rise, not so long ago, up until 2022, Eurozone countries were borrowing at very 
low rates and in some instance, even negative rates. And although debt levels were already at high level at that time, the 
low rates helped manage debt servicing. Not much fiscal effort was needed to keep the public debt ratios in check.  
0:05:55 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Of course, we all remember this because it was a very unusual situation, but now things are very different.

### Page 3

3

0:06:01 - Hélène Baudchon 
Yes, indeed, the situation today is clearly more challenging simply because bond yields are significantly higher and they 
are rising at the time of our discussion. This is for the part the result of the ECB's monetary tightening in 2022 -2023 aimed 
at addressing the inflation shock then. Then long-term interest rates have risen as a consequence and have aligned more 
closely with GDP growth rate. And in technical terms, this is what we refer to as a narrowing of the "r" - "g" spread. 
0:06:41 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Why is this spread so important? Why do you, the economists, monitor it this way?  
0:06:48 - Hélène Baudchon 
Essentially, you have the interest rate "r", which determines the debt burden, while the growth rate "g" determines the 
trajectory of fiscal revenues. And if "r" is lower than "g", and this has been the case for many years, as you can see on the  
chart on the screen, it means that interest payments are increasing at a slower pace than the revenue component of the 
equation. 
0:07:14 - Emmanuel Laborde 
And this provides some leeway in other budgetary areas, such as education, of course, defense, maybe pensions.  
0:07:22 - Hélène Baudchon 
Yes, indeed. And this is what we refer to as the primary budget. However, if and when interest rates do go up while the 
economy isn't growing that much faster, then fiscal leeway diminishes.  
0:07:35 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Crystal clear. Now, just could you provide a few last words to wrap up the sustainability segment?  
0:07:43 - Hélène Baudchon 
Well, to conclude, we are entering a period which demands greater fiscal discipline or, as some might say, less indiscipline 
in order to manage debt trajectories effectively, in particular in the countries which have high debt levels. And for instanc e, 
the Italian case is an interesting one because it is well known for its high debt level ratio. But in fact, it also has an 
advantageous position because it has no primary deficit. In fact, it even benefits from a large primary surplus.  
0:08:22 - Emmanuel Laborde 
As for the countries... For the other countries... I stop you because this will be a discussion with Guillaume Derrien during  
the next segment. Thank you very much, Hélène. 
0:08:30 - Hélène Baudchon 
Thank you, Emmanuel. 
0:08:36 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Hello, Guillaume. Welcome. 
0:08:37 - Guillaume Derrien 
Thank you. 
0:08:38 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Hello, Emmanuel, and hello to everyone. The flexibility of fiscal policy depends heavily on interest rates, which have 
increased considerably over the past few months. The question is, where do we stand today?

### Page 4

4

0:08:52 - Guillaume Derrien 
So, long-term rates have increased in all advanced economies, reaching levels not seen since 2008 in Europe and in the 
United States, and even since 1996 in Japan. Fortunately, the effective rate that states pay on their debt is lower than thos e 
headline figures suggest, simply because the average maturity of debt is relatively long. Precisely how long are we talking 
about? So, it's about 10 years in Japan, 13 years in the United Kingdom, and 8.5 years in France. That means that most of 
the debt was raised before rates started climbing. 
For example, in France, the effective rate just recently rose above the 2% mark, which is significantly lower than the 10 -
year bond yield, which exceeded 4% for new issuances.  
That said, in the United States, which has an average maturity of roughly six years, is experiencing a more rapid increase in  
its effective rate. 
0:09:54 - Emmanuel Laborde 
And this rate increase is happening in the context of high debt -to-GDP ratios and with modest growth, correct? Yes, that's 
correct. 
0:10:05 - Guillaume Derrien 
Debt levels have reached unprecedented heights since the end of World War II. And at the same time, growth is lower, 
hovering around 2% on average in the United States, 1% in Europe and 0.5% in Japan. What does that mean for debt 
servicing costs? So, debt servicing cost is already relatively high, but it is expected to grow further as debt issue before the 
2022 rate hikes mature and are replaced with new instruments bearing higher interest rates. By 2030, interest costs could 
rise to levels last seen in 2000, specifically in France, around 4% of GDP. 
Are all the countries equally affected by these trends? Actually, no. Some countries are managing to reduce their debt 
level. 
Spain, Italy, even Japan have sufficiently improved their primary balance to achieve this, at least for the time being. And o f 
course, we must speak about Germany. Yes, Germany is implementing investment programs that result in a higher public 
deficit and debt-to-GDP ratio. But this ratio started from a relatively low base at around above 60% of GDP. That said, in 
this current context, German bonds have risen. And because German bonds are used as a benchmark for Europe, it  raises 
rates across all Eurozone countries. Is France the only country seeing its public debt go up?  
No, it's not, Emmanuel. This is happening in the United Kingdom and in the United States. These economies, like France, 
are running public deficits that are ever close or exceed 5% of GDP, which is too large for debt ratio to stabilize on its ow n. 
France and the United Kingdom are trying to gradually reduce their public deficit so as not to compromise growth. The 
United States is not going on that path yet, but market pressure could eventually force them to do so.  
0:12:08 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Thank you very much for your input, Guillaume. Thank you, Emmanuel. Let's go to the next segment. It's about the impact 
of rising bond yields on financing and investments in the Eurozone.  
0:12:26 - Thomas Humblot 
Hello, Thomas. Welcome. Hello. Thank you for having me today.  
0:12:30 - Emmanuel Laborde 
We've just seen what's behind the rise in Eurozone bond yields. This rise is partly attributed to the sovereign spread. What 
impact will this have on the economy? 
0:12:42 - Thomas Humblot 
Actually, this widening of some government bond spreads is obviously not good news, but it tends paradoxically to have 
less impact than an increase in long-term interest rates that does not include the sovereign risk premium. for example, in 
France, for a very long time, it has been considered that the 10 -year OAT was the relevant benchmark for fixed loans. And 
at that time, the spread on French government bonds was relatively stable. But since 2024, that spread increased

### Page 5

5

significantly and that renders the sovereign yield a less relevant benchmark.  
And now the most relevant benchmark is the swap rate because it determines the cost of bank funding that backs new 
loans. 
0:13:56 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Since the end of 2024, we have seen a rise in long-term 10-year swap rates. What impact does this have on bank lending 
rates? 
0:14:08 - Thomas Humblot 
Fixed loans have obviously become more expensive, but to a lesser extent than if they were priced solely based on long -
term sovereign rates. And what about existing fixed-rate loans? 
There is no impact. An increase in rates for the entire maturity of a loan does not have any effect if it is a fixed -rate loan. 
Borrowers with outstanding loans are therefore protected. Obviously, new fixed loans are granted at higher rates. And this 
sharply contrasts with a floating-rates loans. 
Because with floating-rates loans, borrowers experience regular rate adjustments throughout the term of the loan. And in 
terms of bank financing? Strictly in terms of bank financing, an increase in short -term rates has an immediate and 
widespread restrictive impact on borrowers, while for long-term interest rates, the impact is more gradual, and it comes 
gradually as the outstanding amount of fixed-rate loans are progressively renewed. And the thing is that since the 
beginning of the war in Iran, we have seen a combination of the two.  
And in practical terms, the rate of new bank loans to non-financial corporations has increased about 25 basis points in the 
eurozone since the beginning of the year. 
0:16:12 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Just a few words about companies that borrow directly from the market.  
0:16:16 - Thomas Humblot 
Well, in that case, the impact is even more straightforward. The rate at which a company borrows from the market is the 
risk-free rate plus a premium. 
0:16:28 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Have risk premiums, known as corporate credit spreads, also increased?  
0:16:34 - Thomas Humblot 
Yes, they have increased temporarily at the beginning of the year but have returned to normal since. For the time being, the 
widening remains moderate for the highest-rated investment-grade issuers. 
However, some significant bond issuance in the euro debt market due to some major US technology companies has 
increased spreads for longer maturities. 
0:17:11 - Emmanuel Laborde 
In particular terms, how does this affect the investment decisions of the companies?  
0:17:19 - Thomas Humblot 
Well, investments that were previously worthwhile due to returns sufficient to cover the cost of capital may no longer do so 
and in turn that might lead to a decrease in demand for financing.  
0:17:40 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Thank you very much Thomas. Now let us move on to the emerging economies. And it is with Cynthia Kalasopatan -Antoine.

### Page 6

6

Hello Cynthia, and welcome. 
0:17:54 - Cynthia Kalasopatan-Antoine 
Hi, Emmanuel. Hello, everyone. 
0:17:57 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Long-term bond yields rose significantly in advanced economies over the summer. What can we now say about emerging 
markets? 
0:18:06 - Cynthia Kalasopatan-Antoine 
Emerging bond markets held up well, which is quite unusual. The rise in long -term sovereign yields in emerging countries 
has been less pronounced than in advanced economies, including the United States and Germany. Now, there are two 
main exceptions, Turkey and Poland. Meanwhile, we did not witness any significant correction in emerging financial 
markets over the summer, and the main currencies appreciated slightly against the dollar or the euro.  
0:18:37 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Does this mean that public finances are more robust in emerging countries?  
0:18:43 - Cynthia Kalasopatan-Antoine 
Well, it depends. You see, in cases like the Czech Republic and South Korea, public debt is moderate and macroeconomic 
fundamentals are solid. However, these countries are now less frequently considered as emerging and tend to be viewed 
as newly advanced economies. By contrast, Brazil and South Africa have weaker public finances and investors have 
already incorporated a higher sovereign risk into their assessments. And this is reflected in the bond yields. And this 
assessment of sovereign risk did not really change over the summer. 
0:19:26 - Emmanuel Laborde 
When comparing the main fiscal metrics, what are the trends? What do you see as an economist?  
0:19:33 - Cynthia Kalasopatan-Antoine 
On average, the fiscal metrics in emerging markets are not substantially worse than those in advanced economies. The 
average budget deficit levels are similar, ranging from 4% to 5% of GDP in 2025 -2026, according to the IMF. And as in 
advanced economies, the public debt is higher than its pre-Covid level. However, the average public debt to GDP ratio in 
emerging countries excluding China is close to 60%, whereas in advanced economies, it is above 100%.  
0:20:12 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Do you arrive at the same conclusion when comparing debt sustainability criteria?  
0:20:17 - Cynthia Kalasopatan-Antoine 
Well, there are significant regional differences. In emerging Asia, the factors that determine debt sustainability are more 
often favorable than those in advanced economies. In Central Europe, the gap between the interest rate paid on debt and 
the GDP growth rate is also favorable. However, the primary deficits in some countries are high.  
0:20:45 - Emmanuel Laborde 
In a few words, a brief overview of the situation in Latin America.  
0:20:49 - Cynthia Kalasopatan-Antoine 
Well, Brazil and Mexico are in a less favorable position. These countries would indeed need to generate a significant 
primary budget surplus each year in order to stabilize their debt in the medium term.

### Page 7

7

0:21:07 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Economists use the term original sin, which refers to the situation when governments resort to borrowing in foreign 
currency to address the shortfall in domestic financing. Does this phenomenon continue to distinguish emerging 
countries? 
0:21:24 - Cynthia Kalasopatan-Antoine 
Yes, this is still the case. Emerging countries still have a significant share of their debt in foreign currency. This repres ents 
about 35 to 40% of their total debt on average. And in fact, now, this figure has decreased over the past decade, and this 
contributes to their resilience. However, governments in emerging markets have a less favorable position as regards their 
debt profile and their financing costs are also on average higher.  
0:22:02 - Emmanuel Laborde 
And finally, just a few words about a larger subject, China.  
0:22:07 - Cynthia Kalasopatan-Antoine 
China's public debt is significant and growing rapidly. That being said, it is denominated in domestic currency and issued in  
domestic markets. This debt is, however, sustainable in the medium term due to a very low effective interest rate and a 
high growth rate. 
0:22:29 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Thank you very much, Cynthia, for being with us today.  
0:22:32 - Cynthia Kalasopatan-Antoine 
Thank you. 
0:22:33 - Emmanuel Laborde 
It is now time to conclude, and we welcome back Group Chief Economist Isabelle Mateos y Lago.  
It is time for our conclusion with Isabelle. Once again, just a final question, please. Could or should, I do not know which 
word is the good one, central banks intervene to curb this rise in interest rates?  
0:22:58 - Isabelle Mateos y Lago 
Absolutely not. The main contribution that central banks can bring to dealing with this situation is to restore price stabili ty, 
as is their mandate, as promptly as possible. And in the current circumstances, this means keeping relatively high policy 
rates, in some cases, rising them further. And the responsibility for keeping public finances on a sustainable footing rests 
squarely with governments and any attempt by these governments to instead take the easy way out by pressuring central 
banks to lower interest rates prematurely would be enormously counterproductive.  
0:23:41 - Emmanuel Laborde 
Thank you very much, Isabelle. Thank you all for joining us today. Just a few words to remind you that additional economic 
research content can be found by scanning the QR code shown on your screen. Do not hesitate to use it with your 
smartphone. It is very easy. It was a pleasure to be with you today. Have a nice day and hope to see you so

《油價與運價維持高檔，航空產業需求遭遇逆風》
近期受到中東衝突升溫導致僅有少量油輪通過荷姆
茲海峽，貨櫃船實際上仍無法通行。過去5-6年全球
港口吞吐運量成長遠低於船舶訂單量成長 ，導致重
大港口的貨櫃塞港嚴重 。中東地緣政治風險和全球
性的港口擁擠預估短期內都未能改善契機 ，因此未
來半年貨櫃運價仍將維持高位 。由於過去半年美國
機票價格已大幅上漲 ，導致價格敏感的美國旅客消
費開始偏向保守。美國運輸安全管理局(簡稱TSA)統
計每日旅客吞吐量近期呈現持續下滑 ，顯示航空業
持續性漲價已經衝擊到旅客需求 。隨著機構上調

4Q26原油預測 10%到88美元，高價位的航空燃油
價格也將再次對全球航空公司運量與獲利造成衝擊 。
《眾議院通過限制擴大對伊朗軍事行動 ，對國防類
股形成壓力》
今年上半年因中東衝突升溫及美伊緊張情勢 ，國防
股曾出現一波強勁上漲 。但隨著美國眾議院 9/15以
220票對204票通過限制總統對伊朗軍事行動的決議
案，要求未獲國會授權前不得持續或擴大對伊朗的
軍事行動 。雖然美國國會決議案主要是限制新增的
軍事行動授權 ，並非削減國防預算 。但美國國會限
制進一步軍事行動 ，已經造成市場資金對國防類股
進行獲利了結 。考量航空類股仍將面臨高油價逆風 ，
以及美國眾議院擴大限制政府對伊朗軍事行動 。本
行對工業產業展望維持中立評等。

## Document 492: インドネシア中銀、市場動揺も、政策運営は政府と市場の「板挟み」が続く ～一部の利上げ観測に「ゼロ回答」、様々な政策目標実_a6041851

Source PDF: all report pdf/インドネシア中銀、市場動揺も、政策運営は政府と市場の「板挟み」が続く ～一部の利上げ観測に「ゼロ回答」、様々な政策目標実_a6041851.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 21 日

1/3 
インドネシア中銀、市場動揺も、政策運営は政府と市場の「板挟み」が続く 
～一部の利上げ観測に「ゼロ回答」、様々な政策目標実現へ難しい対応が続くと予想される～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ インドネシア中銀は 10 月 20～21 日の理事会で、政策金利のＢＩレートを４会合連続で
5.75％に据え置いた。MSCI が市場の透明性やガバナンスへの懸念を背景に 「スタンドアロー
ン市場」への格下げ案を示し、株安・ルピア安が進んだことで、一部では利上げ観測が浮上
していた。しかし、中銀は５～６月の累計100bp の利上げで物価安定には十分との認識を示
し、預金ファシリティ金利を 25bp 引き上げて 5.00％とするとともに、為替ヘッジ取引への
優遇措置を強化し、通貨安定と海外資金の流入促進を図る判断を示した。 
○ 一方で、 景気下支えや雇用創出に向けて、 預金準備率を11～12 月に段階的に引き下げ、 銀行
の貸出余力を拡大する方針を示した。原油高に伴うインフレ圧力や、米国の金融引き締めに
よるドル高圧力を警戒する一方、国内消費の底堅さや民間投資の持ち直し、外需の回復など
を踏まえ、2026 年の経済成長率見通しは「＋4.9～5.7％」との従来の見通しを据え置いた。 
○ 中銀を巡ってはここ数年、政策目標に物価・通貨の安定に加え、経済成長や雇用創出を支援
する役割が追加されてきたほか、政治介入に対する懸念もある。こうしたなか、先行きも政
府の景気下支え要請と金融市場の通貨安定要求との「板挟み」となり、様々な政策目標の実
現を目指す観点から、一段と難しい政策運営を迫られる状況が続くと予想される。 
【インドネシア中銀、市場の混乱で一部に政策金利の引き上げ観測も、４会合連続で据え置き】 
インドネシア銀行（中銀）は、10 月 20～21 日に開催した月例理事会において、政策金利である
ＢＩレートを４会合連続で 5.75％に据え置くことを決定した。一方で、預金ファシリティ金利を
25bp 引き上げ、5.00％とした。中銀は、５月の月例理事会でルピア相場の安定を目的に50bp の大
幅利上げを決定したものの、その後も金融市場ではルピア安の動きが収まらず、株式、債券も下落
するトリプル安に直面した。このため、中銀は６月９日に開催した週次理事会で 25bp の緊急利上
げを実施するとともに、ルピア相場の安定に向けた追加対策を発表した。さらに、６月の月例理事
会でも 25bp の追加利上げを実施し、約１ヵ月の間に累計 100bp もの利上げを行うなど、通貨安定
に向けて金融引き締めを強化した。その後は、ルピア相場が落ち着きを取り戻したことから、前回
の９月会合まで３会合連続で政策金利を据え置き、様子見姿勢を維持してきた。 
しかし、 足元では同国の金融市場が再び混乱に直面している。 きっかけは、 指数算出会社のMSCI

### Page 2

2/3 
が 10 月15 日、同国市場を「スタンドアローン市場」に格下げする案を示し、市場参加者との協議
を開始したことにある（注１）。MSCI は
１月、同国株式市場の情報開示に懸念を

を打ち出し、市場改革を加速させる姿勢
を示した。しかし、その後も市場参加者
からは、協調的な取引行為への疑念が解
消されておらず、実効性のある法執行も
確認できないとの指摘が MSCI に寄せられた。また、フロンティア市場への格下げでは、問題の深
刻さが適切に反映されないとの意見も寄せられた。このため、MSCI は 2027 年４月末まで市場参加
者の意見を募り、 同年６月末までに最終判断を示すとした。 協議を通じて市場のガバナンスや機能
の実質的な改善を裏付ける明確な証拠が確認されない限り、 格下げを進める方針である。 この発表
を受けて、主要株価指数（ジャカルタ総合指数）は一段と下振れしており、ルピア相場も下落して
いる（図１） 。 
こうしたなか、金融市場においては、月例理事会を前に、中銀が政策金利の引き上げを含む市場
安定策を打ち出すとの見方が浮上した。また、中銀は今回、会合後の記者会見の開始時刻を前倒し
すると発表した。中銀では、７月にペリー前総裁が任期を２年弱残して突然辞任し、上級副総裁で
あったデストリ・ダマヤンティ氏が総裁代行を務めた後、９月２日に総裁へ正式就任した。今回の
月例理事会は、デストリ氏の正式就任後２回目の定例会合に当たる。しかし、中銀は今回もＢＩレ
ートの引き上げを見送る一方、 預金ファシリティ金利の引き上げや為替ヘッジ取引への優遇措置の
強化を通じて、市場の安定を図る判断を示した。

【中銀は様々な政策目標の実現に向け、政府と市場の間で「板挟み」となることが予想される】 
会合後に公表した声明文では、 今回の決定について 「イラン情勢の緊迫化や米国の金融引き締め
による対外的な圧力が続くなかでのルピア相場の安定」、「2026 年と 2027 年の物価目標の達成」を
目的とする政策ミックスの一環との考えを示した。一方で、 景気下支えと国内の流動性確保に向け
て、 預金準備率の引き下げを決定した。 一般商業銀行向けは現行の９％から、11 月１日に８％、12
月１日に７％へ、 イスラム銀行向けは現行の7.5％から、11 月１日に6.5％、12 月１日に5.5％へ、
それぞれ段階的に引き下げる。さらに、為替ヘッジ取引への優遇措置を強化し、海外からの資金流

注１ 10 月16 日付レポート「インドネシア株に逆風、MSCI が「スタンドアローン」への格下げ検討」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666610.html）

りません。 
3/3 
入を促す方針を示した。 
世界経済については「イラン情勢の悪化による原油価格の高止まりを受けて、 景気の下押しとイ
ンフレ圧力が併存している」との認識を示す一方、 「2026 年の世界経済成長率は＋３％となる」と
の従来見通しを維持した。 また、 「米国で追加利上げの可能性が高まっているうえ、 財政政策を理由
に米国債金利は高止まりしている」 として、 「新興国への資金流入が抑制され、 ドル高圧力が続く可
能性がある」との見通しを示した。 
一方、 同国経済について 「財政支援や消費者心理の改善を追い風に家計消費は底堅く推移してい
る」としつつ、足元では「企業マインドの改善を背景に民間投資に持ち直しの兆しがでている」と
の認識を示した。先行きは「外需の増加
も見込まれる」として、 「2026 年の経済
成長率は＋4.9～5.7％になる」との従来
見通しを据え置いた。そして、対外収支
も「世界的な不確実性の高まりの影響を
緩和すべく、国際収支を強化する必要が
ある」 としつつ、 「９月末時点の外貨準備
高は輸入の5.3 ヵ月分を有する」とし、
「2026 年の経常赤字はＧＤＰ比▲2.4～
▲1.6％」になると３会合ぶりに見通し
を示した。ルピア相場については「為替
介入やヘッジ取引への優遇措置などを通じた安定強化を図っている」 と従来からの対策を強化する
考えを示した。 そして、 物価動向については 「政府が設定した目標の範囲内で維持されている」 （図
２） ほか、 足元では 「天候不順による食料インフレの懸念が高まっている」ものの、「目標域内に収
めるべく、為替相場の安定に加え、政府も食料インフレ対策を強化する」との見方を示した。 
会合後に記者会見に臨んだ中銀のデストリ総裁は、MSCI による発表について 「株式市場の動向を
注視している」との見方を示した。そのうえで、政策運営について「的を絞ったものにしたい」と
して、 「海外からの投資の呼び込みに資するものにしたい」との考えを示した。そして、 「我々は５
月以降すでに100bp の利上げを実施しており、 物価安定には十分」 、 「海外投資家に対してインセン
ティブを提供している」との考えを示した。先行きの政策運営については「データ次第になる」と
して明言を避けた。また、ソリキン副総裁は、預金準備率引き下げの狙いについて「雇用創出を目
的としたもの」 、 「銀行の貸し出し余力の拡大を目指したもの」と説明した。そして、今回の政策対
応について「様々な政策目的に対応することを目指したもの」と説明した。 
こうした発言の背景には、ここ数年、中銀に対して、物価と通貨の安定に加え、経済成長や雇用
創出を支援する役割も求められるようになっていることがあると考えられる。 政治介入が懸念され
る動きもみられるなか、中銀の政策運営はこれまで以上に難しくなっている。先行きも、中銀は政
府からの景気下支え要請と、金融市場からの通貨安定要求との「板挟み」に置かれるとともに、難
しい政策運営を迫られる局面が続くと予想される。                  以 上

## Document 505: サイバー攻撃による経済損失をどう考えるか ～一企業の被害から、マクロ的な経済損失となるリスク～

Source PDF: all report pdf/サイバー攻撃による経済損失をどう考えるか ～一企業の被害から、マクロ的な経済損失となるリスク～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Economic Trends

2026 年10 月 14 日

1/2 
サイバー攻撃による経済損失をどう考えるか 
～一企業の被害から、マクロ的な経済損失となるリスク～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  前田 和馬 
（℡：050-5473-3888）

情報漏洩を中心にサイバー攻撃による被害が国内外で目立っている。相次ぐサイバー攻撃には
AIが悪用された可能性が指摘されており、今後もこうした被害は拡大する懸念がある。サイバー
攻撃による経済的な影響は主に3 つ考えられる。 
 まず、被害による直接的な金銭損失であり、仮想通貨等の資産の流出、身代金の支払い、被害
者への補償金支払いなどが挙げられる。FBIが被害申告ベースでまとめた「Annual Internet 
Crime Report 2025」によると、米国を中心としたサイバー犯罪の被害総額（個人被害も含む）は
209 億ドルと近年飛躍的に拡大している。なお、こうした被害は基本的に所得移転であり、生産活
動を捕捉する国内総生産（GDP）に直接的な影響を与えるわけではない。 
 次に、サイバー攻撃を通じた企業活動への損害だ。例えばCSISとMcAfeeによる 2020 年の調査で
は、サイバー攻撃を受けた場合に業務は平均18 時間中断したと指摘する。サイバー攻撃は既存顧
客へのサービス提供や新たな顧客獲得活動などを抑制する懸念がある。これに加えて、企業のブ
ランド価値を毀損し、顧客は該当サービスの利用を当面ためらうかもしれない。 
 サイバー攻撃による経済活動への被害が一企業に留まるのであれば、競合他社が代替的にサー
ビスを提供する、或いはシステム復旧後に挽回生産などが生じることで、マクロ的な影響は限定
される。しかし、こうした被害が「交通インフラなど代替が難しい企業」、或いは「産業内の広範
な企業」に及ぶ場合、サイバー攻撃で損なわれた供給能力を補うことが難しく、マクロ経済全体
の損失へと波及する。AIを通じたサイバー攻撃の頻発は、従来のミクロ的な「一企業の被害」か
ら、マクロ的な「産業や経済全体の被害」に転じるリスクを高めている可能性がある。 
 最後に、サイバーセキュリティ対策の強化によるIT関連支出の増加であり、これはGDPを押し上
げる可能性が高い。例えば、2000 年問題（西暦が2000 年になるとコンピュータが誤作動を起こす
との懸念）の際のIT対策費用は全世界で3,000～6,000 億ドルと、1999 年における世界GDPの 0.9
～1.8%に達したとみられている。一方、攻撃をうける企業の防御対策よりも、AI脅威論による開
発抑制の動きが強まる場合、データセンター投資や株式市場の調整を通じて景気減速へと繋がる
懸念がある。なお、米国ではトランプ政権が対中競争力の観点からAI規制に消極的であるほか、
連邦議会におけるFrontier ActなどのAI規制法案も成立に至るかは不透明だ。

### Page 2

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
2/2

出所: FBI より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所が作成

## Document 511: ハンガリーは凍結された復興基金を受け取れるか？ ～「脱オルバン」改革、ＥＵが最終審査～

Source PDF: all report pdf/ハンガリーは凍結された復興基金を受け取れるか？  ～「脱オルバン」改革、ＥＵが最終審査～.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Europe Trends

2026 年 10 月２日

1/2 
ハンガリーは凍結された復興基金を受け取れるか？  
～「脱オルバン」改革、ＥＵが最終審査～ 
 経済調査部 首席エコノミスト  田中 理 
（℡：050-5474-7494）

（要旨） 
○ ハンガリーは９月30 日、 欧州復興基金の最終支払い請求を欧州委員会に提出した。 オルバン
前政権下では、司法の独立性や汚職、公共調達の透明性などを巡る懸念から資金提供が凍結
されてきたが、４月の政権交代後、マジャル政権はＥＵとの関係正常化に向けた改革を急速
に進めてきた。 新たな復興計画は総額約100 億ユーロで、 新政権は８月末までに必要な改革 ・
投資条件を達成したとしている。 ただ、 今回の申請によって資金受領が確定した訳ではなく、
欧州委員会が条件の達成状況を審査したうえで年末までに支払いの可否を判断する。巨額の
ＥＵ資金を受領できるかは、新政権による「脱オルバン」改革の実効性と、悪化したＥＵと
の関係修復の進展を占う試金石となる。

ハンガリーは９月30 日、欧州復興基金の最終支払い請求を欧州委員会に提出した。復興基金
は、コロナ危機からの経済復興に必要な財政資金を加盟国に提供する目的で2021 年に創設され、
南欧や東欧諸国に手厚く配分されてきた。復興基金を通じた財政支援は、①資金の一部を、返済
を前提とする融資ではなく、返済が不要な補助金として提供する点や、②気候変動対策、デジタ
ル化対応、ロシア産化石燃料依存の脱却といった欧州連合（ＥＵ）の優先課題、各国固有の構造
問題などに取り組むことを追加の資金受領の条件としている点が、従来の財政支援の枠組みと異
なる。復興基金からの追加の資金提供は2026 年末で終了する。追加の資金受領を希望する国は、
８月 31 日までに復興計画で合意した改革条件を達成し、それに基づいて、９月30 日までに支払
い請求をしなくてはならなかった。 
ハンガリーはオルバン前首相の治世下で、法の支配など欧州連合（ＥＵ）の基本価値、ロシア
制裁、ウクライナ支援などを巡って、ＥＵとの対立を繰り返してきた。司法の独立性、汚職問
題、公共調達の透明性などへの懸念があるとして、ＥＵはハンガリーに対する復興基金からの資
金提供を凍結してきた。資金提供を開始するには、司法の独立性やＥＵ予算の保護などに関する
27 項目の条件（これをスーパー・マイルストーンと呼ぶ）を達成する必要があった。状況が大き
く変わったのは、今年４月の総選挙で親ＥＵ政権が誕生したことだ（詳細は４月13 日付けレポー
ト「ハンガリーに親ＥＵ政権誕生へ」を参照されたい）。マジャル首相は５月にフォンデアライエ
ン欧州委員長と会談し、凍結されてきたＥＵ資金の解除に向けた改革に取り組むことを約束し

### Page 2

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
2/2 
た。ハンガリー政府は前政権が提出した復興計画の見直しに着手し、６月に新たな計画を提出、
ＥＵはこれを７月に正式に承認した。 
新たな復興計画は、返済不要の補助金が約65 億ユーロ、返済が必要な融資が約 35 億ユーロの
総額約 100 億ユーロで、資金受領の条件として67 項目の改革と47 項目の投資が盛り込まれた。
新政権は発足直後から改革に着手し、公共放送の経営・編集体制の刷新、前政権が設置した治安
機関の廃止、汚職やＥＵ資金を監視する欧州検察庁への参加を決定したほか、７月には基本法
（憲法に相当）を改正し、①前政権が選出した大統領の早期退任、②憲法裁判所判事の70 歳定年
制の復活、③憲法裁判所による予算や税制関連法に対する審査権限強化、④最高裁判所や司法行
政トップの選出方法変更と任期短縮、⑤首相や国家議員の任期制限、⑥国家資産の回収・保護局
の創設、⑦議会の３分の２以上の賛成が必要な法律の範囲を縮小するなどの改革に着手した（詳
しくは８月12 日付けレポート「ハンガリーで急速に進む「脱オルバン」改革」を参照された
い） 。政府は８月末に、復興基金の受領に必要な条件を期限内に達成したと発表していた。 
今回の申請により、ハンガリーが復興基金からの資金を受領できると確定した訳ではない。欧
州委員会は提出された資料を基に、復興計画で記された定量・定性目標が達成されたかどうかを
判断する。条件を満たしていないと判断された場合、支払いが留保されたり、減額されたりする
可能性がある。条件未達と判断された部分について、来年以降に改革を追加して改めて資金を請
求することは基本的にできない。新たな復興計画は約100 億ユーロの総額のうち、事前資金とし
て受け取った部分を除く、約91 億ユーロが今回の最終審査の対象となる。欧州委員会は駆け込み
申請をした他の加盟国からの支払い請求とともに、11 月20 日頃までに暫定評価をまとめ、閣僚理
事会などの意見を踏まえ、12 月末までに支払い決定を行う予定だ。９月だけで21 ヶ国から総額
1,054 億ユーロの請求が提出されており、審査期間は極めて短い。 
 ハンガリーにとって今回の支払い請求は、単に約91 億ユーロのＥＵ資金を確保できるかを左右
するだけではない。オルバン前政権下で長年にわたりＥＵとの対立の火種となってきた法の支配
や汚職対策を巡り、４月に発足した新政権が僅か数ヶ月で進めた改革を、ＥＵがどこまで十分と
認めるかが本格的に試される。巨額のＥＵ資金の行方とともに、オルバン時代に悪化したＥＵと
の関係修復がどこまで進んだのかを占ううえでも、欧州委員会の審査結果が注目される。

## Document 519: ロシアの物価状況（26年9月）－総合インフレ率は6％台前半で推移

Source PDF: all report pdf/ロシアの物価状況（26年9月）－総合インフレ率は6％台前半で推移.pdf
Report source: NLI
Date: 2026-10-15
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1． 結果の概要：総合指数、コア指数ともに前年比でわずかに上昇 
10月9日、ロシア連邦統計局は消費者物価指数を公表し、結果は以下の通りとなった。 
【総合指数（26年9月）】 
・前年同月比は6.35％、市場予想1（6.30％）から上振れ、前月（6.33％）からやや上昇（図表1） 
・前月比は0.35％、市場予想（0.30％）から上振れ、前月（▲0.08％）からプラスに転じる 
【コア指数2（26年 9月）】 
・前年同月比は5.47％、前月（5.37％）から上昇した（図表2） 
・前月比は0.49％、前月（0.29％）から上昇した 
（図表 1）

（図表 2）

2． 結果の詳細：直近は 10月 1日の公共料金引き上げが物価を大きく押し上げ 
9 月のロシアのインフレ率は 6.35％となり、8月（6.33％）からごくわずかに上昇、市場予想
（6.30％）から上振れた。ロシア中銀による季調済前月比の年率換算は6.5％となり、8月（7.5％）
から減速した。 
前年比インフレ率を大分類別に見ると、9月は食料品が 5.05％（前月：5.08％）、財（非食料品）
が 6.47％（前月：6.51％）、サービスが 7.97％（前月：7.85％）となり、食料品と財（非食料品）
でやや低下、サービスでやや上昇した。 
前年比寄与度では食料品が 2.0％ポイント程度、財（非食料品）が 2.1％ポイント程度、サービ

1 Bloomberg集計の中央値。以下の予想値も同様。 
2 生鮮食品など季節的要因による影響を受ける品目や管理品目を除いた指数。

ロシアの物価状況（26年 9月） 
－総合インフレ率は 6％台前半で推移

### Page 2

（図表 4）

ロシア中央銀行が公表する家計のインフレ期待（1年先中央値、実際のインフレ率よりも高めに
なる傾向がある）は、9月は14.2％で 8月（13.7％）から上昇した。期待インフレ率と実際のイン
フレ率の乖離は過去の傾向（期待インフレ率≒前年比インフレ率＋6％、図表5）と比較してやや開
いており、9月は前月からさらに拡大した。 
品目別の上昇率を見ると3（図表 6）、9月は前
年比でガソリン（ 22.71％）、保険サービス
（18.73％）、グラニュー糖（ 17.38％）、卵
（17.20％）の伸び率が高い一方、バター（▲
7.96％）は相対的にマイナス幅が大きかった。
また前月比では、教育サービス（8.71％） 、卵
（8.61％）の上昇率が高く、青果物（▲4.24％）、
その他サービス（▲3.88％）のマイナス幅が大
きかった。 
各品目の消費ウエイトも考慮して、全体のイ
ンフレ率への寄与を品目別に見ると（図表 7・8） 、前年比上昇率へのプラス寄与が大きい品目はガ
ソリン（1.02％ポイント） 、家庭サービス（0.61％ポイント）だった。また、前月比上昇率への寄与
では、プラス寄与では教育サービス（約 0.11％ポイント） 、マイナス寄与では青果物（約▲0.20％

記載。

## Document 543: 中金：如何对冲AI风险？

Source PDF: all report pdf/中金：如何对冲AI风险？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

中⾦认为，除配置现⾦、中债、⾼股息股票等防御性资产外，美元贬值交易同样可对冲 AI
⻛险 ——AI 降温将削弱美元需求，⼆者负相关可形成天然对冲。增配⻩⾦是执⾏该交易的
最优⼯具， 2026 年⻩⾦与美元相关系数⾼达 -81.2% ，且历史上美元下跌期间⻩⾦上涨胜率
达 92.3% ，显著优于美债。
摘要：
中⾦研究
从 AI 交易交易到 “AI 美元 ” ，美元成为全球股市分化的 “ 分界线 ”
在国际货币秩序重构背景下，我们认为 “ 去美元化 ” 仍是⼤势所趋，美元⾯临⻓期贬值压⼒（《去
美元化结束了吗？》）。但 2026 年以来，美元⾛势阶段性偏强，除地缘冲突引发通胀压⼒外， AI
⾏情可能提供关键⽀撑。⼀⽅⾯，先进芯⽚、云服务和算⼒等 AI 产业链跨境交易较多采⽤美元结
算，产业扩张可能阶段性增加美元需求，形成所谓 “AI 美元 ” 效应。另⼀⽅⾯， AI 投资增强相关经
济体的增⻓动能，尤其是美国，预计 2026 年实际 GDP 增量近三成来⾃ AI 。经济增⻓韧性为美联
储加息创造条件，美债利率上升推⾼美元。但对于 AI 受益程度较低的经济体，美元⾛强带来的外

部融资条件收紧和资本流出压⼒，反⽽会拖累经济与市场表现。
在上述机制影响下，美元成为 AI ⾰命中全球股市分化⾏情的分界线：2026 年以来，深度参与 AI
产业链的美国、⽇本和韩国股市与美元正相关，⽽ AI 受益相对有限的印度股市、恒⽣指数则与美
元负相关。
图表 1 ： 2026 年以来，⽇经 225 指数与美元呈正相关
资料来源： Wind ，中⾦公司研究部
图表 2 ： 2026 年以来，韩国 KOSPI 指数与美元呈正相关
我们仍看好 AI 资产回报前景，建议通过资产配置应对近期市场波动，为 AI 资产 “ 购买保险 ” 。在现
⾦、债券和⾼股息股票等防御性资产外，美元贬值交易同样有助于对冲 AI 资产⻛险，增配⻩⾦或
为该交易的有效策略。

AI 交易波动加⼤，除增配防御性资产外，美元贬值交易也可作为对冲选择
尽管 AI ⾰命仍处于早期阶段，但市场已开始担忧头部⼚商的增⻓放缓，以及未来回报能否覆盖当
前的⼤规模投⼊。市场预计，美国 AI 七巨头资本开⽀同⽐增速将由 2026 年的约 90% 降⾄ 2027 年
的 36% ，投资回报的不确定性也加剧了 AI 资产波动。
图表 6 ：市场预期 2027 年 AI 资本开⽀仍将增⻓，但增速明显放缓

### Page 4

资料来源： Bloomberg ，中⾦公司研究部
在此背景下，增配现⾦、中债和⾼股息股票等防御性资产具有合理性。在此之外，根据前⽂分
析， AI 交易降温可能削弱美国相对增⻓优势及美元需求，美元贬值交易亦有望对冲 AI 资产的调整
压⼒。
美元贬值交易的另⼀优势在于，其获利情景并不限于 AI ⾏情转弱：如果美伊局势缓和推动油价回
落，或美国通胀明显改善、促使美联储转向降息， AI 资产受益于流动性改善，美元贬值交易从中
受益，此时 AI 股票也可能同步上涨。因此，相⽐单纯减持 AI 资产，布局美元贬值交易既可保留
AI 上涨的收益敞⼝，也可在多种情景下提供组合保护。
这⼀对冲逻辑的主要⻛险是美元流动性危机。如果 AI ⾏情剧烈调整引发全球资产集中抛售，投资
者争相变现、获取美元现⾦，美元可能⾛强，与股票反向波动，对冲能⼒失效。但当前 AI ⾏情调
整主要体现为投资回报担忧引发的⻛险偏好下降和资产再配置，尚未演变为⽆序的流动性冲
击，美元与科技股更可能正相关。近期美股科技股调整期间，美元指数亦从 10 ⽉ 14 ⽇⾼位回

落，与这⼀判断相符。
上述分析主要聚焦汇率与股市的短期关系；拉⻓时间看，即使 AI ⾰命成功，股票上涨也未必伴随
美元升值，原因在于 “ 逆巴萨效应 ” 带来实际汇率贬值压⼒（详⻅《 AI 冲击下的 “ 逆巴萨效应 ” 》）。
⻩⾦是布局美元贬值交易的有效⼯具，建议增配
布局美元贬值交易，最直接的⽅式是做空美元，但外汇交易⾯临诸多限制，增配⻩⾦或是更便
利的策略表达。从理论上看，⻩⾦作为美元体系的替代性资产，与美元存在负相关的基础，但
由于⾦价同时受到利率、通胀和避险需求等多重因素影响，这⼀关系在历史上并不始终稳定。
但进⼊ 2026 年，⻩⾦与美元负相关性明显增强，相关性升⾄ -81.2% 。美元与⻩⾦成为⼀个硬币
的两⾯，让增配⻩⾦成为美元贬值交易的有效⼯具。
图表 7 ： 2026 年以前，⻩⾦与美元相关性并不稳定

主导⾦价⾛势，美债收益率上⾏也未能始终压制⻩⾦表现。

除⻩⾦外，美债也是布局美元贬值交易的潜在选择，但胜率可能低于⻩⾦：如果美元贬值源于
通胀回落和美联储降息，⻩⾦与美债均可能受益；如果美元贬值源于美国债务⻛险上升或美元
信⽤动摇，⻩⾦可能受益于替代性资产需求，美债却可能因期限溢价上升⽽下跌。
历史复盘也⽀持上述判断。我们复盘了 2000 年以来持续⾄少 14 天的全部 91 段美元下跌区间，⻩
⾦上涨 84 次，胜率达到 92.3% ，区间收益率中位数为 5.46% ；同期 2 年期和 10 年期美债收益率下
降的⽐例分别为 68.1% 和 64.8% 。

## Document 547: 付加価値税は一旦引き下げると再引き上げは難しいか､あらためて考える ～タイでは時限措置が30年以上継続､税財政改革を含めた冷_0ed86994

Source PDF: all report pdf/付加価値税は一旦引き下げると再引き上げは難しいか､あらためて考える ～タイでは時限措置が30年以上継続､税財政改革を含めた冷_0ed86994.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年 10 月 6 日

1/3 
付加価値税は一旦引き下げると再引き上げは難しいか､あらためて考える 
～タイでは時限措置が 30 年以上継続､税財政改革を含めた冷静な議論を期待したい～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ 日本では、 政府が2027 年４月から２年間、 飲食料品の消費税率を８％から１％に引き下げる
法案を提出している。 政府は2029 年４月に税率を元に戻す方針だが、 「一度下げた税率を引
き上げるのは難しい」との指摘もある。こうした点を考えるうえで、タイのＶＡＴ（付加価
値税）の事例は示唆的である。 
○ タイでは 1992 年にＶＡＴを導入し、1997 年に税率を 10％へ引き上げた。しかし、アジア通
貨危機後の景気悪化を受け、1999 年に１年間の時限措置として実効税率を７％へ引き下げ
た。 その後、 この措置は繰り返し延長され、 今年７月には2027 年９月末までの延長が決まっ
た。背景には、景気低迷やエネルギーインフレ、洪水被害などを受け、家計負担の軽減や個
人消費の下支えを図る狙いがある。 
○ 一方、ＶＡＴは主要な税収源であり、少子高齢化や人口減少に伴う社会保障費の増加を踏ま
えれば、財政の持続可能性確保のため増税の必要性は高まっている。政府も将来的に税率を
段階的に10％へ戻す方向性を示しているが、 景気や世論への配慮から実際には引き上げに踏
み切れず、時限措置が長期化している。 
○ このため、日本でも消費税率の引き下げを時限措置とする場合、予定通り税率を元に戻せる
のかという問題を含め、タイの経験を十分に考慮する必要がある。

10 月５日に第222 臨時国会が召集された。 今国会では、 政府が提出した、 飲食料品への消費税を
８％から１％に引き下げることを柱とする関連法案が審議される。 同法案では、 消費税について 「現
下の物価の上昇に鑑み臨時的に２年間に限り引き下げる」 と明記し、2027 年４月から２年間に限定
する方針が示された。高市首相も、 「２年後に私が責任をもって確実に税率を元に戻していく」 と述
べている。一方、今年２月に実施された衆議院総選挙において、与党・自民党は税率をゼロとする
ことを公約に掲げたため、 １％ポイントに相当する額について、 所得に連動した新たな現金給付制
度を導入する方針を盛り込んだ。加えて、税率を元に戻す 2029 年４月に給付付き税額控除制度を
導入すべく、 制度設計を本格化させるとしている。 なお、 時限措置としての消費税減税については、

### Page 2

2/3 
「一度引き下げると引き上げるハードルが高い」との議論がある。こうしたなか、あらためてタイ
で実施されたＶＡＴ（付加価値税）の時限的な引き下げについて紹介する。 
タイでは長らく、商取引の各段階で売上高全体に対する営業税が課されてきた。しかし、経済の
国際化が進展するなかで、 二重課税が生じることが課題とされてきた。このため、 タイ政府は1992
年、産業構造の転換と近代化を目的に、営業税に代わり、付加価値部分にのみ課税するＶＡＴを導
入した。なお、負担者は最終消費者であるものの、登録事業者に納税義務があるほか、インボイス
方式が採用されており、 この点で日本の消費税と類似している。導入当初の税率は７％（国税6.3％、
地方税 0.7％）であったが、1997 年に10％（国税９％、地方税１％）へ引き上げられた。こうした
なか、タイは 1997～98 年に発生したアジア通貨危機の発端となり、深刻な景気悪化に直面した。
これを受けて、タイ政府は 1999 年に「１年間の時限措置」として、ＶＡＴの実効税率を７％に引
き下げる決定を行った。当初は１年間の時限措置とされたものの、 実際にはその後も勅令の公布な
どにより措置は繰り返し延長されてきた。政府は今年７月にも措置を１年延長する勅令を承認し、
これにより実行税率を７％とする時限措置は2027 年９月末まで継続されることとなった。 
政府がＶＡＴの引き下げ措置を再び延長した背景には、 家計の負担軽減と国内消費を下支えする
狙いがある。タイでは、 イラン情勢の悪化を受けたエネルギーインフレが足かせとなり、 ４-６月の
実質ＧＤＰ成長率は前期比年率▲0.74％と３四半期ぶりのマイナス成長となるなど、 景気にブレー
キが掛かっている （注１）。このため、 仮にＶＡＴの引き下げ措置を終了して税率を10％に戻せば、
幅広く財・サービス価格に一段の上昇圧力がかかると予想される。政府としては、家計の実質購買
力への悪影響を回避し、個人消費の下振れを防ぐべく、 現行税率を維持する必要性が高まったと捉
えられる。さらに、９月中旬以降の豪雨による洪水被害が拡大しており、景気のさらなる下振れが
懸念される状況にある（注２）。 
一方、ＶＡＴの引き下げ措置が長期化していることには課題もある。ＶＡＴは税収の約３割を占
める主要な税収源であり、現在７％に抑えられている実効税率を法定税率である10％に戻せば、 理
論上は歳入の増加につながる余地がある。このため、過去には、税率引き下げを終了して法定税率
に戻す議論が度々行われた。2024 年末には、 当時のペートンタン政権のピチャイ副首相兼財務相が
税率の引き上げに言及したものの、世論の反発を受け 、ペートンタン首相が否定した経緯もある。
とはいえ、タイでは、 急速な少子高齢化にコロナ禍が重なり 2020 年を境に人口減少局面に突入し、
社会保障費の増大による財政負担が増加するなど、 中長期的な財政の持続可能性の観点から税財政
改革が急務となっている。一方で、タイ経済は ASEAN（東南アジア諸国連合）主要国のなかでコロ
ナ禍からの景気回復が最も遅れており、政府にとって、家計負担の増大に加え、観光関連産業への
悪影響も懸念される増税の判断は下しにくい。仮に実効税率を廃止する場合でも、 給付金や補助金、

注１ ８月17 日付レポート「タイ景気に急ブレーキ､内需低迷が堅調な外需を相殺」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/645327.html） 
注２ 10 月２日付レポート「タイ、洪水被害が景気の新たな重石に」 
  （https://www.dlri.co.jp/report/macro/666229.html）

### Page 3

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
3/3
低所得者対策、物価対策など総合的な政策パッケージを準備する必要がある。その意味では、既存
の制度を変更せず７％の税率を維持する方が追加的な政策コストを抑えやすいことも、 時限措置が
長期化する一因になっていると考えられる。
財政の持続可能性向上が喫緊の課題となるなか、上院（元老院）では、ＶＡＴを段階的に法定税
率に戻す案などを提案する動きがみられた。 政府も、 実効税率を段階的に8.5％まで引き上げ、2030
年までに10％とする方向性を示している。 しかし、 現実には前述の通り、 ７％に引き下げる暫定措
置の延長に動いている。日本において、今国会で展開される議論についても、一度引き下げた税率
を予定通り元に戻すことの難しさを含め、こうしたタイの経験を十分に勘案する必要があろう。

Report source: 东吴证券

Pages: 34

### Page 1

证券研究报告·宏观报告·宏观深度报告

东吴证券研究所

2026 年 10 月 20 日

证券分析师  芦哲 
执业证书：S0600524110003

相关研究

[Table_Tag]    [Table_Summary] 模型设计：五大维度、12 个指标的因子打分框架 
◼ 宏观择时的落地痛点：理论研究在实际投资中面临调仓时间固定、 参数
与因子过拟合、宏观数据处理失真三大痛点。 我们对比因子打分、因子
回归、事件冲击和美林时钟四类常见模型后， 选择逻辑透明、对样本依
赖较小的因子打分模型。 
◼ 五大维度、12 个指标：模型从基本面 （经济、 货币、 信用） 、 资金面 （杠
杆、期货、期权） 、国际面（利差、汇率） 、估值面（ERP）和技术面五
个维度选取 12 个指标，每个指标给出+1 或-1 的信号，五个维度的权重
分别为 30%、30%、20%、10%和 10%，综合评分区间为-10 至+10 分。 
◼ 指标筛选兼顾逻辑与定量：每个入选指标既要有清晰的经济学逻辑， 又
要在训练集中满足胜率高于 50%、 超额收益尽量高的要求； 信号规则只
与上月值、 近3 个月均值或历史分位数比较， 不针对单个指标优化参数，
以降低过拟合风险。 
回测表现：弱信号合成强信号，样本外表现稳健 
◼ 训练集胜率接近 70%：入选单因子在训练集中的胜率大多在 55%左右，
五个维度合成后，训练集（2016 年 1 月—2023 年 9 月）方向判断胜率
提升至 69.89%。按综合评分≥0 分持有万得全 A、<0 分空仓回测，年
化收益为 17.67%，同期万得全 A 为-0.54%，年化超额收益为 18.20%；
最大回撤为 8.16%，远低于万得全 A 的 35.76%。 
◼ 样本外表现稳健：测试集 （2023 年 10 月—2026 年 9 月） 方向判断胜率
为 66.67%，与训练集基本一致；年化收益为22.76%，同期万得全 A 为
9.26%， 年化超额收益为13.50%； 最大回撤为11.04%， 低于万得全A 的
20.97%。全样本年化超额收益为 16.96%。 
◼ 熊市防守能力突出：2016 年至 2026 年（2026 年为 1—9 月）的 11 个年
份中， 模型有9 年跑赢万得全 A， 且在万得全A 下跌的 5 个年份中均取
得正收益。 综合评分≥3 分时， 万得全A 下月上涨的概率为 77.78%；≤
-5 分时仅为 17.65%。 
◼ 模型局限：模型适合中长期配置，但月频调仓难以适应 A 股的高波动，
对部分极端行情反应较慢， 如2024 年 10 月上旬的快速回调， 以及2025
年 7 月和 2026 年 4 月的快速上涨。 
最新观点：2026 年 10 月模型评分为-1 分 
◼ 各维度评分：基本面（-2 分）是主要拖累，信用扩张动能偏弱；估值面
（-1 分） 显示股票相对债券的性价比尚不突出； 资金面 （+1 分） 和技术
面（+1 分） 偏多， 杠杆资金有所降温， 趋势有所改善； 国际面多空相抵
（0 分） 。 
◼ 市场展望：2016 年 1 月至 2026 年 9 月，综合评分为-1 分的情形共出现
14 次，次月万得全 A 上涨的概率为 57.14%，但下跌时的平均跌幅
（4.77%）明显大于上涨时的平均涨幅（1.59%） 。我们认为10 月 A 股
以震荡为主，后续重点关注信用数据能否企稳回升。 
◼ 风险提示：模型基于历史数据构建， 存在失效风险； 宏观数据存在发布
滞后与修订； 海外政策、 地缘冲突等不可控外力可能导致模型短期失效。

### Page 5

5 / 34 
1.   宏观择时研究的落地痛点与模型设计思路 
资产配置与择时领域的研究成果丰富， 但许多回测表现亮眼的模型， 在实际投资中
很难取得理想效果。 本章首先梳理理论研究落地过程中的三类痛点， 再对比市面上常见
的四类权益月度择时模型， 说明本文选择因子打分框架的原因， 最后介绍模型的总体结
构。 
1.1.   理论研究在实际投资中的痛点 
资产配置理论研究在实际投资中存在诸多痛点， 导致很多看起来不错的理论研究在
实际操作层面难以取得理想效果。 我们将其归纳为调仓时间、 模型稳健性和宏观数据处
理三个方面。 
1.1.1.   调仓时间固定 
资产配置模型多由金工量化团队完成。为便于回测，多数量化研究采用月频调仓，
调仓时点固定，常见的有月初、月末和月中（例如每月5 日，以等待部分月初经济数据
更新） 。 
而实际投资中盘面变化较快， 投资经理很少只在每月固定的时间点进行交易。 固定
时点的月频信号对月内的突发行情反应滞后，与实际投资的决策节奏存在错位。因此，
本文模型定位于研判下个月的大盘方向，而非给出精确的交易时点。 
1.1.2.   参数与因子过拟合 
部分量化研究为了得到较为好看的回测结果， 会刻意堆砌数据进行测算， 容易出现
严重的共线性或参数过拟合问题。 还有一些指标并没有显著的经济学意义， 与市场走势
的相关性可能只是历史上的巧合。 
这类模型往往样本内表现亮眼，样本外却失效过快。我们认为，指标入选首先应当
具备清晰的经济学逻辑；其次，信号规则应尽量简单、参数尽量少，并通过样本外检验
确认其稳健性。 
1.1.3.   宏观数据处理失真 
资产配置研究中，除纯量化的金工研究外，还有大量宏观主观研究，而部分量化研
究对宏观数据特性的理解仍有待深入。例如，部分宏观数据存在春节效应，统计上会将
1 月、2 月数据合并发布，但部分研究统一将数据滞后一个月处理，与实际的数据发布
节奏严重不符。 
此外， 政策的公布往往会引起宏观预期的快速变化， 而依赖已公布数据的量化研究，
很难及时跟上预期的改变。因此，构建宏观因子时，需要按真实的发布时间对齐数据，
并结合宏观逻辑处理特殊月份。

### Page 6

6 / 34 
1.2.   常见月度择时模型对比与选择 
市面上常见的权益月度择时模型主要有四种类型。 每类模型都有自身特色， 也都在
部分时期取得过较好的回测效果。 
1.2.1.   四类常见模型：因子打分、因子回归、事件冲击、美林时钟 
因子打分模型：寻找经济学逻辑较强的有效宏观因子，从多个维度进行评测挖掘，
最后进行综合打分。 
因子回归模型：寻找多个与大盘指数相关性较高的因子进行回归测算， 得到下一期
（通常为下一个月）的预期收益率。 
事件冲击模型：将宏观因子的变化转化为事件因子，例如PMI 连续多期上升、CPI
连续多期下降后转升等， 寻找历史上有效的事件冲击因子， 分析正负冲击力度后进行综
合打分。 
美林时钟模型：根据经济增长与通胀对经济周期进行划分， 在不同阶段配置不同资
产，进而达到择时配置的目的。 
表1：常见权益月度择时模型对比 
模型类型 核心思路 模型输出 主要特点 
因子打分

总 
综合评分 
可解释性强，可拆解各维
度贡献 
因子回归 多因子回归预测下期收益 预期收益率

参数不稳定影响 
事件冲击 识别宏观数据的特定变化形态

数据来源 ：东吴证券研究所整理 
1.2.2.   选择因子打分模型的原因 
经过综合测算和考量，我们最终选择因子打分模型，主要基于以下三点考虑。 
第一，经济学解释力度较好。每个因子都对应清晰的经济逻辑， 模型结论可以拆解
到具体维度，能够清楚看到当期是哪个维度对判断影响较大。 
第二，适合“弱信号合成强信号” 。大盘指数受多重因素共同影响，单个宏观指标
对其解释力度天然有限。 从我们的测试看， 多数有效指标单独使用时的胜率仅在55%附
近；若按 t 检验、信息比率等传统统计标准筛选，几乎没有指标能够入选。因子打分模
型并不要求单个因子具有很强的预测能力， 而是将一批具备经济学逻辑、 胜率稳定高于
50%的指标综合起来，合成胜率显著更高的综合信号。 
第三，适合持续更新改进。打分规则简单透明，新增或替换某一维度的指标不会影

### Page 7

7 / 34 
响整体框架，便于在跟踪过程中持续迭代。 
1.3.   模型总体框架：五大维度、权重与打分规则 
本模型以万得全 A 指数为择时标的， 按月频调仓。 模型从基本面、 资金面、 国际面、
估值面、技术面五个维度对市场进行评测，各维度包含若干子指标。 
指标选取标准。指标选取遵循两类标准： 一是经济学逻辑，指标本身必须具有明确
的经济含义，能够解释其影响权益市场的方向；二是定量检验，单个指标的择时胜率需
大于 50%，同时超额收益率尽量高。 
打分规则。打分规则并非事先设定，而是在测算中逐步确定的。我们对每个候选指
标测试了不同的信号构建方式，例如与上月值比较、与近3 个月均值比较、计算历史分
位数等，并检验信号方向与经济逻辑是否一致，最终只保留检验有效的指标和规则。入
选的每个子指标给出+1 或-1 的信号， 其中基本面的经济、 信用维度各包含两个指标， 每
个指标占 0.5 分。 
综合评分。各维度得分直接加总，得到-10 分至+10 分的综合评分。各维度的分值
上限由最终入选的有效指标数量决定，即基本面 3 分（30%） 、资金面3 分（30%） 、国
际面 2 分（20%） 、估值面1 分（10%） 、技术面1 分（10%） 。综合评分≥0 分时，模型
看好下个月万得全 A 指数的方向；综合评分<0 分时，模型看空下个月的方向。 
回测区间。2015 年“811 汇改” 后，海外因素对A 股的影响明显增大， 因此模型测
算从 2016 年开始。模型的回测结果详见第 8 章。

2.   基本面维度：经济、货币、信用 
基本面维度从经济、货币、信用三个子维度刻画宏观环境，满分3 分，占综合评分
的 30%。 我们在各子维度内测试了多个候选指标， 并对每个指标比较了不同的信号构建
方式，最终入选 5 个指标：经济维度的社会消费品零售总额当月同比、规模以上工业增
加值当月同比， 货币维度的货币环境指数， 以及信用维度的金融机构中长期贷款余额同
比和 M2 同比。 
信号构建方式。对于每个候选指标， 我们主要比较四种信号构建方式： 当日指标信
号，即当月值与上月值比较；均值指标信号，即当月值与近3 个月均值比较；当日分位
指标信号和均值分位指标信号， 即分别以当月值、 近3 个月均值在历史滚动窗口中所处
的分位数生成信号。 
单因子检验口径。本章单因子检验的统计区间为训练集， 即2016 年 1 月至 2023 年
9 月，共 93 个月（样本划分详见第 7 章） 。每月末根据信号判断下个月万得全A 指数的
涨跌方向：信号为正或为零时看多，为负时看空。胜率为方向判断正确的月份占比；年
化超额收益为按信号持有万得全 A（看空时空仓） 相对于一直持有万得全A 的年化收益
之差。模型在测试集上的表现，将在第 8 章结合综合信号统一展示。 
2.1.   经济维度 
经济维度反映实体经济的景气程度， 满分1 分， 由社零和工业增加值两个指标构成，
每个指标占 0.5 分。 
2.1.1.   指标选择：为什么选社零和工业增加值 
经济维度共测试了 9 个候选指标，覆盖需求、生产、景气调查、价格、能耗和地产
六类数据 （表3） 。 社零和工业增加值分别对应需求端和生产端， 是统计局月度数据中覆
盖面最广、 市场关注度最高的两项实体经济指标。 在训练集中， 社零的胜率为56.99%，
年化超额收益为 4.59%；工业增加值的胜率为 59.14%，年化超额收益为 6.40%，两者均
满足胜率高于 50%、超额收益较高的标准。 
其余候选指标未入选的原因主要有三类。 一是胜率接近50%：PMI 在四种构建方式
下的胜率均不超过 52.69%， 择时能力有限。 二是表现不及入选指标： 价格类指标 （CPI、
PPI、CPI−PPI） 具备一定的择时能力， 但训练集胜率最高为55.91%， 均低于入选的社零
和工业增加值。三是数据质量或收益贡献不足：全社会用电量在训练集中胜率较高
（59.14%） ，但数据更新时间不稳定，不利于每月按时生成信号；二手住宅价格同比的
胜率为 58.06%，但年化超额收益仅 2.67%，环比指标的胜率则不超过 53.76%。

工业增加值同比 均值指标信号 59.14% 6.40% 入选

CPI 同比 均值指标信号 55.91% 4.57% 不及入选指标

注：落选指标列示训练集胜率最高的信号构建方式。

2.1.2.   信号规则与逆周期逻辑 
经济维度的两个指标均采用均值指标信号， 且为逆向规则： 当月同比低于近3 个月
均值时，信号为+1；当月同比高于近 3 个月均值时，信号为-1。 
为什么是逆向规则？直觉上，经济数据走强应当利好股市，但测算结果恰恰相反：
若采用顺向规则 （当月同比高于近3 个月均值时看多） ， 训练集中社零的胜率仅为41.94%，
工业增加值仅为 40.86%，均明显低于 50%；采用逆向规则后，胜率分别提升至 56.99%
和 59.14%。我们认为逆向关系主要来自以下三个方面。 
一是政策预期。我国宏观调控具有明显的逆周期特征。经济数据边际走弱时，市场
对稳增长政策加码的预期升温，降准降息、财政发力等政策预期会提升风险偏好，对权
益市场形成支撑；经济数据持续走强时，政策进一步加码的必要性下降，甚至可能面临
政策退坡的担忧。 
二是市场的领先性。股票价格反映的是对未来盈利的预期，通常领先于经济数据。
经济数据仍然偏弱时，市场往往已经开始交易修复预期；而当经济数据明显好转、同比
读数持续走高时，增长预期大多已被定价，继续上行的空间反而有限。 
三是流动性的再分配。实体经济需求偏弱时， 企业扩张意愿下降， 资金在实体部门
的回报降低，更多流向金融资产，推升权益市场估值；经济过热时，实体部门对资金的
需求上升，流动性对金融资产的支撑相对减弱。 
在数据处理上，社零和工业增加值存在春节效应：统计局将 1 月、2 月数据合并发
布， 每年1 月末、2 月末均没有新的当月数据， 需要等到3 月中旬 1—2 月合计数据公布
后才能更新信号。 模型严格按照数据的实际发布时间生成信号， 不使用信号日之后才公
布的数据。

2.2.   货币维度 
货币维度反映货币政策的松紧程度，满分 1 分，由货币环境指数单一指标构成。 
2.2.1.   指标选择：以货币政策感受指数为锚 
衡量货币政策松紧最直接的指标是中国人民银行 《银行家问卷调查报告》 中的货币
政策感受指数，它反映银行家对当期货币政策松紧的判断。但该指数为季度数据，且发
布存在滞后，无法直接用于月频择时。 
因此，我们以货币政策感受指数为锚，选取月度及更高频的利率数据，构建与其走
势相近的月频货币环境指数。 降准、 降息与公开市场操作是货币政策最直接的表达方式，
基础指标共 12 个，可分为三类：一是准备金率，包括大型和中小型存款类金融机构的
存款准备金率；二是政策利率，包括7 天逆回购利率、1 年期 MLF 利率、1 年期和 5 年
期 LPR，以及 1 年期存款和贷款基准利率；三是市场利率，包括 DR007、R007 以及 1
年期和 10 年期国债到期收益率。 
2.2.2.   货币环境指数的构建 
我们将上述基础指标合成为月频的货币环境指数， 用于刻画货币政策的边际松紧变
化。 
需要说明的是，近年来我国货币政策框架处于持续调整之中。2025 年 2 月之前，
MLF 利率是重要的中期政策利率，此后 MLF 逐步转变为工具型利率，其政策信号意义
有所下降。 与此同时， 央行陆续推出了国债买卖、 买断式逆回购等新的流动性管理工具。
这些新工具对市场流动性的影响确实重要，但由于推出时间较短、历史数据不足，暂未
纳入货币环境指数，后续将随着数据的积累择机纳入。

### Page 11

11 / 34 
在四种信号构建方式中， 当日指标信号的效果最好 （表4） ： 当月货币环境指数较上
月边际宽松时， 信号为+1； 边际收紧时， 信号为-1。 训练集中， 该信号的胜率为58.06%，
年化超额收益为 7.67%。 
值得注意的是， 基于水平比较的两种方式 （胜率58.06%、56.99%） 明显优于基于分
位数的两种方式（胜率均为 52.69%） 。这说明对权益市场而言，货币政策的边际变化比
货币环境所处的历史位置更重要。

2.3.   信用维度 
信用维度反映实体部门的融资需求和信用扩张情况， 满分1 分， 由中长期贷款余额
同比和 M2 同比两个指标构成，每个指标占 0.5 分。 
2.3.1.   指标选择：为什么选中长期贷款和 M2 
信用维度共测试了 5 个候选指标 （表5） 。 中长期贷款主要投向企业资本开支和居民
购房，能较好地反映实体部门的真实融资需求，是信用扩张“质量”的体现；M2 衡量
全社会的广义流动性，是信用扩张“总量”的体现。训练集中，中长期贷款余额同比的
胜率为 59.14%，年化超额收益为 10.05%；M2 同比的胜率为 59.14%，年化超额收益为

### Page 12

12 / 34 
6.41%，两者的胜率在候选指标中并列最高。 
社会融资规模存量同比与中长期贷款高度重叠（人民币贷款是社融的主要构成部
分） ，且训练集胜率最高为55.91%，未入选。M1 同比及 M1−M2 剪刀差的年化超额收
益偏低，均不超过 4.45%，也未入选。

M2 同比 当日指标信号 59.14% 6.41% 入选

2.3.2.   信号规则 
中长期贷款余额同比采用均值指标信号： 当月同比高于近3 个月均值时， 信号为+1；
低于近 3 个月均值时，信号为-1。M2 同比采用当日指标信号：当月同比高于上月时，
信号为+1；低于上月时，信号为-1。中长期贷款数据为余额口径，我们根据余额计算同
比后再生成信号。 
与经济维度不同，信用维度采用顺势规则：信用数据走强，意味着宽松政策已开始
向实体部门传导，企业和居民的融资需求回升，盈利改善的预期随之增强。经济维度与
信用维度从两个角度刻画了“政策底—市场底”的过程：前者捕捉政策加码的预期，后
者确认政策传导的效果。 
图3：信用维度单因子择时净值（训练集， 2016 年 1 月—2023 年 9 月）

数据来源：Wind，东吴证券研究所

3.   资金面维度：杠杆、期货、期权 
资金面维度从杠杆资金、 期货资金和期权资金三个角度刻画市场的资金行为与情绪，
满分 3 分，占综合评分的 30%。三个子维度各选取一个指标，每个指标占1 分：两市融
资余额、沪深 300 股指期货前 20 名会员净持仓，以及上证 50ETF 期权的持仓量认沽认
购比（PCR）。 
信号构建方式。资金类数据多为日频，因此在第2 章四种方式的基础上，我们进一
步区分“当日值”与“月均值”两种取数口径，共比较六种信号构建方式：当日指标信
号， 即月末当日值与上月末比较； 均值指标信号_日月， 即月末当日值与当月均值比较；
均值指标信号_月环比，即当月均值与上月均值比较；均值指标信号_月均值，即当月均
值与近 3 个月均值比较； 以及当日分位指标信号和均值分位指标信号。 单因子检验口径
与第 2 章一致，统计区间为训练集（2016 年 1 月至 2023 年 9 月） 。 
候选指标。资金面共测试了 6 个候选指标 （表6） 。 除最终入选的3 个指标外， 杠杆
资金方面还测试了融资买入额和融资净流入额， 外资方面测试了北向资金。 融资买入额
的最高胜率为 59.14%， 但年化超额收益为3.83%， 低于融资余额； 融资净流入额的结果
则不够稳定：胜率最高的构建方式年化超额收益仅为2.64%，而年化超额收益最高的构
建方式胜率仅为 52.69%，接近 50%。北向资金在训练集中表现尚可，但自2024 年 8 月
起不再披露每日资金流向数据，无法持续生成信号，因此未纳入模型。 
表6：资金面候选指标单因子表现（训练集）  
指标 信号构建方式 胜率 年化超额 结论 
融资余额 均值指标信号_月均值 55.91% 5.24% 入选 
股指期货净持仓 均值指标信号_月环比 59.14% 7.73% 入选 
PCR 当日指标信号 53.76% 5.70% 入选 
融资买入额 均值指标信号_月均值 59.14% 3.83% 超额收益不及融资余额 
融资净流入额 均值分位指标信号 56.99% 2.64% 结果不稳定 
北向资金 均值指标信号_月均值 55.91% 6.16% 数据停止披露 
数据来源：Wind，东吴证券研究所 
注：落选指标列示训练集胜率最高的信号构建方式。

信号方向。资金面三个指标中， 融资余额和股指期货净持仓采用逆向规则，PCR 采
用顺势规则。 这背后的逻辑是： 杠杆资金和期货持仓反映的是资金的 “拥挤程度” ， 过度
集中往往意味着情绪已经充分释放，后续容易反转；而期权 PCR 反映的是市场情绪的
边际变化， 情绪改善的趋势在一个月的维度上更容易延续。 三个入选指标在六种构建方
式下的表现见表 7。

3.1.   杠杆资金：融资余额 
融资余额是投资者通过融资融券向券商借入资金买入股票的未偿还余额， 代表杠杆
资金的规模，是衡量市场风险偏好的常用指标。与融资买入额、融资净流入额等流量指
标相比，融资余额是存量指标，受单日交易波动的影响较小，能够更稳定地反映杠杆资
金的整体水平。 
信号规则。融资余额采用均值指标信号_月均值，且为逆向规则：当月融资余额的
月均值低于近 3 个月月均值时，信号为+1；高于近 3 个月月均值时，信号为-1。训练集
中， 该信号的胜率为55.91%， 年化超额收益为5.24%， 在六种构建方式中超额收益最高。 
为什么是逆向规则？ 若采用顺向规则（融资余额上升时看多） ，训练集胜率仅为
44.09%。 融资资金具有明显的趋势追随特征， 往往在市场上涨后加速流入。 融资余额快
速攀升，说明杠杆资金趋于拥挤、市场情绪偏热，一旦行情转向，去杠杆带来的抛压会
放大调整；反之，融资余额回落意味着杠杆资金已经出清，市场的潜在抛压有所减轻，
后续反弹的空间更大。

### Page 15

15 / 34 
图4：杠杆资金单因子择时净值（训练集， 2016 年 1 月—2023 年 9 月）

数据来源：Wind，东吴证券研究所 
3.2.   期货资金：沪深 300 股指期货前 20 名净持仓 
股指期货是机构投资者管理权益仓位风险的主要工具。我们选取上市时间最长、 流
动性最好的沪深 300 股指期货作为测算标的， 统计前20 名会员的多头持仓与空头持仓，
计算净持仓（多头持仓减空头持仓） 。前 20 名会员以机构为主，其持仓变化能够反映机
构对冲需求和多空情绪的变化。 
信号规则。股指期货净持仓采用均值指标信号_月环比，且为逆向规则：当月净持
仓的月均值低于上月月均值时，信号为+1；高于上月月均值时，信号为-1。训练集中，
该信号的胜率为 59.14%，年化超额收益为 7.73%，在资金面候选指标中表现最好。 
为什么是逆向规则？若采用顺向规则，训练集胜率仅为40.86%。前 20 名会员的净
持仓通常为净空头，主要来自机构的套期保值需求。净持仓下降、即净空头扩大，说明
机构加大了对冲力度，市场的悲观预期较为集中，这类时点往往接近阶段性底部；净空
头收窄则说明对冲需求下降、乐观情绪逐步积累，市场的上行空间反而受限。

### Page 16

16 / 34 
图5：期货资金单因子择时净值（训练集， 2016 年 1 月—2023 年 9 月）

数据来源：Wind，东吴证券研究所 
3.3.   期权资金：上证 50 期权 PCR 
PCR（Put-Call Ratio ）即认沽期权持仓量与认购期权持仓量之比，是衡量期权市场
多空情绪的常用指标。 我们选取上市时间最长的上证50ETF 期权作为测算标的， 数据始
于 2015 年 2 月：PCR = 认沽持仓量 / 认购持仓量。 
信号规则。PCR 采用当日指标信号， 且为顺势规则： 当月最后一个交易日的PCR 低
于上月最后一个交易日时，信号为+1；高于上月时，信号为-1。训练集中，该信号的胜
率为 53.76%，年化超额收益为 5.70%，在六种构建方式中超额收益最高。 
逻辑解释。PCR 下降说明认沽持仓相对减少、 认购持仓相对增加， 投资者买入下跌
保护的需求下降， 市场情绪边际改善；PCR 上升则说明避险需求增加。 与杠杆资金和期
货持仓不同， 期权持仓的变化更多反映情绪的边际方向， 在一个月的维度上具有一定的
延续性。若采用逆向规则，训练集胜率仅为 46.24%。

4.   国际面维度：利差与汇率 
国际面维度刻画海外因素对 A 股的影响， 满分2 分， 占综合评分的20%， 由中美利
差和美元兑离岸人民币汇率两个指标构成，每个指标占 1 分。 
2015 年“811 汇改”之后，人民币汇率形成机制的市场化程度明显提高，叠加沪深
港通、债券通等互联互通机制不断完善、A 股纳入全球主要指数，海外资金和全球流动
性对 A 股的影响持续增强。中美利差和人民币汇率是联系国内外资本市场的两个核心
价格： 前者反映中国资产相对于美国资产的收益吸引力， 后者反映跨境资金流动的方向
和对人民币资产的信心。这也是模型测算从 2016 年开始的原因之一。 
国际面共测试了 3 个候选指标 （表8） 。 除入选的两个指标外， 我们还测试了期限利
差。 期限利差在训练集中的胜率最高为56.99%， 但对应的年化超额收益仅为0.08%，择
时贡献有限，未纳入模型。信号构建方式与第3 章相同，共比较六种方式，单因子检验
的统计区间为训练集（2016 年 1 月至 2023 年 9 月） 。
